FILE: EthData\MarketData.py

import argparse
import json
import math
import sys
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd
import requests


class KrakenMarketData:
    ASSET_PAIRS_URL = (
        "https://api.kraken.com/0/public/AssetPairs"
    )

    OHLC_URL = (
        "https://api.kraken.com/0/public/OHLC"
    )

    DEFAULT_PAIR = "ETHUSD"
    DEFAULT_INTERVAL = 1440
    REQUEST_TIMEOUT = 30

    VALID_INTERVALS = {
        1,
        5,
        15,
        30,
        60,
        240,
        1440,
        10080,
        21600,
    }

    REQUEST_HEADERS = {
        "User-Agent": "EthStatistics/1.0",
        "Accept": "application/json",
    }

    ASSET_ALIASES = {
        # Bitcoin
        "XXBT": "BTC",
        "XBT": "BTC",
        "BTC": "BTC",

        # Ethereum
        "XETH": "ETH",
        "ETH": "ETH",

        # Litecoin
        "XLTC": "LTC",
        "LTC": "LTC",

        # XRP
        "XXRP": "XRP",
        "XRP": "XRP",

        # Dogecoin
        "XXDG": "DOGE",
        "XDG": "DOGE",
        "DOGE": "DOGE",

        # Other legacy Kraken symbols
        "XETC": "ETC",
        "ETC": "ETC",
        "XMLN": "MLN",
        "MLN": "MLN",
        "XREP": "REP",
        "REP": "REP",

        # Fiat currencies
        "ZUSD": "USD",
        "USD": "USD",
        "ZEUR": "EUR",
        "EUR": "EUR",
        "ZGBP": "GBP",
        "GBP": "GBP",
        "ZJPY": "JPY",
        "JPY": "JPY",
        "ZCAD": "CAD",
        "CAD": "CAD",
        "ZAUD": "AUD",
        "AUD": "AUD",
        "ZCHF": "CHF",
        "CHF": "CHF",
    }

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._session = requests.Session()

        self._session.headers.update(
            self.REQUEST_HEADERS
        )

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._session.close()

    def __enter__(self) -> "KrakenMarketData":
        return self

    def __exit__(
        self,
        exception_type: Any,
        exception_value: Any,
        traceback: Any,
    ) -> None:
        self.close()

    # ==========================================
    # METADATA
    # ==========================================

    def get_metadata(
        self,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        payload = self._get_json(
            self.ASSET_PAIRS_URL
        )

        result = payload.get("result")

        if not isinstance(result, dict):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned no asset-pair metadata."
            )

        markets: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        for internal_pair, pair_data in result.items():
            market = self._create_market_metadata(
                internal_pair=internal_pair,
                pair_data=pair_data,
            )

            if market is not None:
                markets.append(
                    market
                )

        markets = self._remove_duplicate_markets(
            markets
        )

        markets.sort(
            key=lambda market: (
                market["quoteAsset"],
                market["baseAsset"],
                market["displayName"],
            )
        )

        if not markets:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned no usable online markets."
            )

        quote_currencies = sorted(
            {
                market["quoteAsset"]
                for market in markets
                if market["quoteAsset"]
            }
        )

        return {
            "success": True,
            "error": None,
            "markets": markets,
            "intervals": self._create_intervals(),
            "periods": self._create_periods(),
            "quoteCurrencies": quote_currencies,
        }

    def _create_market_metadata(
        self,
        internal_pair: str,
        pair_data: Any,
    ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        if not isinstance(pair_data, dict):
            return None

        status = str(
            pair_data.get(
                "status",
                "",
            )
        ).strip().lower()

        if status and status != "online":
            return None

        rest_pair = str(
            pair_data.get(
                "altname",
                internal_pair,
            )
        ).strip().upper()

        if not rest_pair:
            return None

        web_socket_name = str(
            pair_data.get(
                "wsname",
                "",
            )
        ).strip().upper()

        base_asset, quote_asset = (
            self._extract_assets(
                web_socket_name=web_socket_name,
                pair_data=pair_data,
            )
        )

        if not base_asset or not quote_asset:
            return None

        # Always construct the display name from the
        # normalized assets. This converts XBT/USD
        # into BTC/USD while retaining XBTUSD as the
        # REST pair sent to Kraken.

        display_name = (
            f"{base_asset}/{quote_asset}"
        )

        return {
            "pair": rest_pair,
            "displayName": display_name,
            "baseAsset": base_asset,
            "quoteAsset": quote_asset,
        }

    # ==========================================
    # OHLCV DATA
    # ==========================================

    def get_ohlcv(
        self,
        pair: str,
        interval: int = DEFAULT_INTERVAL,
        include_current_candle: bool = False,
    ) -> pd.DataFrame:
        normalized_pair = self._validate_pair(
            pair
        )

        normalized_interval = (
            self._validate_interval(
                interval
            )
        )

        payload = self._get_json(
            self.OHLC_URL,
            params={
                "pair": normalized_pair,
                "interval": normalized_interval,
            },
        )

        result = payload.get("result")

        if not isinstance(result, dict):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned no OHLC result."
            )

        pair_keys = [
            key
            for key in result
            if key != "last"
        ]

        if not pair_keys:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned no OHLC series."
            )

        raw_candles = result.get(
            pair_keys[0]
        )

        if not isinstance(raw_candles, list):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned OHLC data "
                "in an unexpected format."
            )

        if not raw_candles:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned an empty OHLC series."
            )

        dataframe = pd.DataFrame(
            raw_candles,
            columns=[
                "timestamp",
                "open",
                "high",
                "low",
                "close",
                "vwap",
                "volume",
                "trade_count",
            ],
        )

        # Kraken's last OHLC row is the currently
        # incomplete candle. Exclude it by default.

        if (
            not include_current_candle
            and not dataframe.empty
        ):
            dataframe = (
                dataframe.iloc[:-1].copy()
            )

        if dataframe.empty:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned no completed candles."
            )

        dataframe["date"] = pd.to_datetime(
            dataframe["timestamp"],
            unit="s",
            utc=True,
            errors="raise",
        )

        decimal_columns = [
            "open",
            "high",
            "low",
            "close",
            "vwap",
            "volume",
        ]

        dataframe[decimal_columns] = (
            dataframe[decimal_columns]
            .apply(
                pd.to_numeric,
                errors="raise",
            )
            .astype(float)
        )

        dataframe["trade_count"] = (
            pd.to_numeric(
                dataframe["trade_count"],
                errors="raise",
            )
            .astype("int64")
        )

        return (
            dataframe[
                [
                    "date",
                    "open",
                    "high",
                    "low",
                    "close",
                    "vwap",
                    "volume",
                    "trade_count",
                ]
            ]
            .sort_values(
                by="date",
                ascending=True,
            )
            .reset_index(
                drop=True
            )
        )

    # ==========================================
    # HTTP
    # ==========================================

    def _get_json(
        self,
        url: str,
        params: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            response = self._session.get(
                url,
                params=params,
                timeout=self.REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
            )

            response.raise_for_status()

        except requests.Timeout as error:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "The Kraken request timed out."
            ) from error

        except requests.ConnectionError as error:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken could not be reached. "
                "Check the internet connection."
            ) from error

        except requests.HTTPError as error:
            status_code = (
                error.response.status_code
                if error.response is not None
                else "unknown"
            )

            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned HTTP status "
                f"{status_code}."
            ) from error

        except requests.RequestException as error:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Kraken request failed: {error}"
            ) from error

        try:
            payload = response.json()

        except requests.JSONDecodeError as error:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned invalid JSON."
            ) from error

        self._validate_kraken_payload(
            payload
        )

        return payload

    # ==========================================
    # METADATA DEFINITIONS
    # ==========================================

    @staticmethod
    def _create_intervals(
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [
            {
                "name": "1 Minute",
                "minutes": 1,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "5 Minutes",
                "minutes": 5,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "15 Minutes",
                "minutes": 15,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "30 Minutes",
                "minutes": 30,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "1 Hour",
                "minutes": 60,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "4 Hours",
                "minutes": 240,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "1 Day",
                "minutes": 1440,
                "isDefault": True,
            },
            {
                "name": "1 Week",
                "minutes": 10080,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "15 Days",
                "minutes": 21600,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _create_periods(
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [
            {
                "name": "30 Days",
                "days": 30,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "90 Days",
                "days": 90,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "180 Days",
                "days": 180,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
            {
                "name": "1 Year",
                "days": 365,
                "isDefault": True,
            },
            {
                "name": "Maximum",
                "days": 720,
                "isDefault": False,
            },
        ]

 

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_kraken_payload(
        payload: Any,
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken returned an unexpected "
                "response format."
            )

        errors = payload.get(
            "error",
            [],
        )

        if errors:
            error_message = ", ".join(
                str(error)
                for error in errors
            )

            raise RuntimeError(
                "Kraken API error: "
                f"{error_message}"
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_pair(
        pair: str,
    ) -> str:
        if pair is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "The trading pair is required."
            )

        normalized_pair = (
            str(pair)
            .strip()
            .upper()
        )

        if not normalized_pair:
            raise ValueError(
                "The trading pair is required."
            )

        return normalized_pair

    def _validate_interval(
        self,
        interval: int,
    ) -> int:
        try:
            normalized_interval = int(
                interval
            )

        except (TypeError, ValueError) as error:
            raise ValueError(
                "The interval must be an integer."
            ) from error

        if (
            normalized_interval
            not in self.VALID_INTERVALS
        ):
            valid_intervals = ", ".join(
                str(value)
                for value in sorted(
                    self.VALID_INTERVALS
                )
            )

            raise ValueError(
                "Unsupported Kraken interval: "
                f"{normalized_interval}. "
                "Valid values: "
                f"{valid_intervals}."
            )

        return normalized_interval

    # ==========================================
    # MARKET NORMALIZATION
    # ==========================================

    @classmethod
    def _extract_assets(
        cls,
        web_socket_name: str,
        pair_data: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> tuple[str, str]:
        if "/" in web_socket_name:
            parts = web_socket_name.split(
                "/",
                maxsplit=1,
            )

            base_asset = cls._normalize_asset(
                parts[0]
            )

            quote_asset = cls._normalize_asset(
                parts[1]
            )

            return (
                base_asset,
                quote_asset,
            )

        base_asset = cls._normalize_asset(
            str(
                pair_data.get(
                    "base",
                    "",
                )
            )
        )

        quote_asset = cls._normalize_asset(
            str(
                pair_data.get(
                    "quote",
                    "",
                )
            )
        )

        return (
            base_asset,
            quote_asset,
        )

    @classmethod
    def _normalize_asset(
        cls,
        asset: str,
    ) -> str:
        normalized = (
            asset
            .strip()
            .upper()
        )

        return cls.ASSET_ALIASES.get(
            normalized,
            normalized,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _remove_duplicate_markets(
        markets: list[dict[str, Any]],
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        unique_markets: dict[
            str,
            dict[str, Any]
        ] = {}

        for market in markets:
            pair = str(
                market.get(
                    "pair",
                    "",
                )
            ).strip().upper()

            if not pair:
                continue

            unique_markets.setdefault(
                pair,
                market,
            )

        return list(
            unique_markets.values()
        )



def safe_json_number(
    value: Any,
) -> float:
    number = float(value)

    if not math.isfinite(number):
        raise ValueError(
            "A candle contains a non-finite "
            "numeric value."
        )

    return number


def dataframe_to_market_response(
    dataframe: pd.DataFrame,
    pair: str,
    interval: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    candles = [
        {
            "date": row.date.isoformat(),
            "open": safe_json_number(
                row.open
            ),
            "high": safe_json_number(
                row.high
            ),
            "low": safe_json_number(
                row.low
            ),
            "close": safe_json_number(
                row.close
            ),
            "vwap": safe_json_number(
                row.vwap
            ),
            "volume": safe_json_number(
                row.volume
            ),
            "tradeCount": int(
                row.trade_count
            ),
        }
        for row in dataframe.itertuples(
            index=False
        )
    ]

    return {
        "success": True,
        "error": None,
        "pair": pair.strip().upper(),
        "interval": int(interval),
        "candles": candles,
    }


def create_market_error_response(
    error: Exception,
    pair: str,
    interval: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "success": False,
        "error": str(error),
        "pair": str(pair).strip().upper(),
        "interval": int(interval),
        "candles": [],
    }


def create_metadata_error_response(
    error: Exception,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "success": False,
        "error": str(error),
        "markets": [],
        "intervals": [],
        "periods": [],
        "quoteCurrencies": [],
    }


def print_json(
    response: dict[str, Any],
) -> None:
    print(
        json.dumps(
            response,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            ensure_ascii=False,
            allow_nan=False,
        ),
        flush=True,
    )



def parse_arguments(
) -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Kraken metadata and daily OHLC "
            "service for EthStatistics."
        )
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--mode",
        choices=[
            "metadata",
            "ohlcv",
        ],
        default="ohlcv",
        help=(
            "Execution mode. "
            "Default: ohlcv."
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--pair",
        type=str,
        default=KrakenMarketData.DEFAULT_PAIR,
        help=(
            "Kraken REST pair. "
            "Default: ETHUSD."
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--interval",
        type=int,
        default=KrakenMarketData.DEFAULT_INTERVAL,
        choices=sorted(
            KrakenMarketData.VALID_INTERVALS
        ),
        help=(
            "Candle interval in minutes. "
            "Default: 1440 for daily candles."
        ),
    )

    parser.add_argument(
        "--include-current-candle",
        action="store_true",
        help=(
            "Include Kraken's current "
            "incomplete candle."
        ),
    )

    return parser.parse_args()


# ==========================================
# APPLICATION ENTRY POINT
# ==========================================

def main() -> int:
    args = parse_arguments()

    try:
        with KrakenMarketData() as service:
            if args.mode == "metadata":
                response = service.get_metadata()

            else:
                dataframe = service.get_ohlcv(
                    pair=args.pair,
                    interval=args.interval,
                    include_current_candle=(
                        args.include_current_candle
                    ),
                )

                response = (
                    dataframe_to_market_response(
                        dataframe=dataframe,
                        pair=args.pair,
                        interval=args.interval,
                    )
                )

        print_json(
            response
        )

        return 0

    except Exception as error:
        if args.mode == "metadata":
            response = (
                create_metadata_error_response(
                    error
                )
            )

        else:
            response = (
                create_market_error_response(
                    error=error,
                    pair=args.pair,
                    interval=args.interval,
                )
            )

        print_json(
            response
        )

        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(
        main()
    )

<Project Sdk="Microsoft.NET.Sdk">

	<PropertyGroup>
		<OutputType>WinExe</OutputType>
		<TargetFramework>net10.0-windows10.0.26100.0</TargetFramework>
		<Nullable>enable</Nullable>
		<ImplicitUsings>enable</ImplicitUsings>
		<UseWPF>true</UseWPF>
		<AllowUnsafeBlocks>true</AllowUnsafeBlocks>
		<Platforms>AnyCPU;x64</Platforms>
	</PropertyGroup>

	<ItemGroup>
		<None Include="$(MSBuildProjectDirectory)\..\EthData\MarketData.py">
			<Link>Python\MarketData.py</Link>
			<CopyToOutputDirectory>PreserveNewest</CopyToOutputDirectory>
		</None>
	</ItemGroup>

	<ItemGroup>
	  <PackageReference Include="ExcelDataReader" Version="3.9.0" />
	  <PackageReference Include="Microsoft.Identity.Client" Version="4.92.0" />
	  <PackageReference Include="Microsoft.Identity.Client.Broker" Version="4.92.0" />
	</ItemGroup>

	<Target Name="CopyQuantEngine" AfterTargets="Build">

		<Copy SourceFiles="$(MSBuildProjectDirectory)\..\QuantEngine\x64\$(Configuration)\QuantEngine.dll" DestinationFolder="$(TargetDir)" SkipUnchangedFiles="true" />

	</Target>

</Project>

============================================================
FILE: EthStatistics\MainWindow.xaml.cs
============================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Interop;
using System.Windows.Media;
using EthStatistics.Models;
using EthStatistics.Services;
using EthStatistics.Settings;
using EthStatistics.Views;
using Microsoft.Identity.Client;
using EthStatistics.Services;

namespace EthStatistics
{
    public partial class MainWindow : Window
    {
        // ==========================================
        // CONSTANTS
        // ==========================================

        private const int DailyInterval =
            1440;

        private const int DefaultPeriodDays =
            365;

        private const string DefaultBaseAsset =
            "ETH";

        private const string DefaultQuoteCurrency =
            "USD";


        // ==========================================
        // SERVICES
        // ==========================================

        private readonly KrakenMarketDataService
            _marketDataService = new();

        private readonly UserPreferencesService
            _preferencesService = new();

        private readonly ThemeService
            _themeService = new();

        private readonly AuthenticationService
            _authenticationService = new();


        // ==========================================
        // STATE
        // ==========================================

        private UserPreferences
            _preferences = new();

        private UserSession
            _currentUser = UserSession.Guest();

        private CancellationTokenSource?
            _refreshCancellation;

        private SignedOutView?
            _signedOutView;

        private bool
            _signInInProgress;

        private bool
            _allowClose;


        private string? CurrentPreferenceAccountId
        {
            get
            {
                return _currentUser.IsAuthenticated
                    ? _currentUser.AccountId
                    : null;
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CONSTRUCTOR
        // ==========================================

        public MainWindow()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            Loaded +=
                MainWindow_Loaded;

            Closing +=
                MainWindow_Closing;

            StateChanged +=
                MainWindow_StateChanged;

            SourceInitialized +=
                MainWindow_SourceInitialized;

            MarketDataStore.Instance.Changed +=
                MarketDataStore_Changed;


            ShowSignedOutView();
            Loaded += MainWindow_Loaded_CheckForUpdates;

        }


        // ==========================================
        // WINDOW INITIALIZATION
        // ==========================================

        private void MainWindow_SourceInitialized(
            object? sender,
            EventArgs e)
        {
            EnterFullScreen();
        }

        private async void MainWindow_Loaded_CheckForUpdates(
    object sender,
    RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            Loaded -=
                MainWindow_Loaded_CheckForUpdates;

            var updateService =
                new UpdateService();

            await updateService
                .CheckAndDownloadUpdateAsync();
        }
        private void EnterFullScreen()
        {
            WindowState =
                WindowState.Normal;

            Left =
                0;

            Top =
                0;

            Width =
                SystemParameters.PrimaryScreenWidth;

            Height =
                SystemParameters.PrimaryScreenHeight;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // WINDOW CONTROLS
        // ==========================================

        private void MinimizeButton_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            WindowState =
                WindowState.Minimized;
        }


        private void MaximizeButton_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            WindowState =
                WindowState ==
                WindowState.Maximized
                    ? WindowState.Normal
                    : WindowState.Maximized;
        }


        private void CloseButton_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            Close();
        }


        private void MainWindow_StateChanged(
            object? sender,
            EventArgs e)
        {
            MaximizeButton.Content =
                WindowState ==
                WindowState.Maximized
                    ? "❐"
                    : "□";
        }


        // ==========================================
        // STARTUP
        // ==========================================

        private async void MainWindow_Loaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            // Do not automatically authenticate.
            // Every application launch starts
            // signed out.

            _currentUser =
                UserSession.Guest();


            await LoadCurrentUserPreferencesAsync();


            UpdateUserInterface();


            EnterSignedOutState();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // USER PREFERENCES
        // ==========================================

        private async Task LoadCurrentUserPreferencesAsync()
        {
            _preferences =
                await _preferencesService.LoadAsync(
                    CurrentPreferenceAccountId
                );


            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    _preferences.QuoteCurrency
                )
            )
            {
                _preferences.QuoteCurrency =
                    DefaultQuoteCurrency;
            }


            if (
                !_preferences.PeriodDays.HasValue
                ||
                _preferences.PeriodDays.Value <= 0
            )
            {
                _preferences.PeriodDays =
                    DefaultPeriodDays;
            }


            // Remove this assignment if you remove
            // IntervalMinutes from UserPreferences.

            _preferences.IntervalMinutes =
                DailyInterval;


            _themeService.ApplyTheme(
                _preferences.Theme
            );
        }


        private async Task SaveCurrentUserPreferencesAsync()
        {
            // Remove this assignment if you remove
            // IntervalMinutes from UserPreferences.

            _preferences.IntervalMinutes =
                DailyInterval;


            await _preferencesService.SaveAsync(
                _preferences,
                CurrentPreferenceAccountId
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // SIGNED-OUT STATE
        // ==========================================

        private void EnterSignedOutState()
        {
            CancelMarketRefresh();


            DataSourceText.Text =
                "—";


            DataStatusText.Text =
                "Sign in to load market data";


            DataStatusIndicator.Fill =
                FindBrush(
                    "TextMutedBrush"
                );


            UseApiDataButton.Visibility =
                Visibility.Collapsed;


            UpdateUserInterface();


            ShowSignedOutView();
        }


        private void ShowSignedOutView()
        {
            _signedOutView =
                new SignedOutView();


            _signedOutView.SignInRequested +=
                SignedOutView_SignInRequested;


            MainContent.Content =
                _signedOutView;
        }


        private async void SignedOutView_SignInRequested(
            object? sender,
            EventArgs e)
        {
            await SignInAsync();
        }


        private void CancelMarketRefresh()
        {
            _refreshCancellation?.Cancel();

            _refreshCancellation?.Dispose();

            _refreshCancellation =
                null;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // SIGN IN
        // ==========================================

        private async Task SignInAsync()
        {
            if (_signInInProgress)
            {
                return;
            }


            _signInInProgress =
                true;


            _signedOutView?.SetLoading(
                true
            );


            ShowLoadingStatus(
                "Signing in..."
            );


            try
            {
                IntPtr windowHandle =
                    GetWindowHandle();


                UserSession signedInUser =
                    await _authenticationService
                        .SignInAsync(
                            windowHandle
                        );


                if (!signedInUser.IsAuthenticated)
                {
                    _currentUser =
                        UserSession.Guest();

                    EnterSignedOutState();

                    return;
                }


                _currentUser =
                    signedInUser;


                UpdateUserInterface();


                await LoadCurrentUserPreferencesAsync();


                MarketDataStore.Instance
                    .ClearImportedData();


                await LoadMetadataAsync();


                if (_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
                {
                    ShowDashboard();
                }
            }
            catch (MsalClientException error)
                when (IsAuthenticationCancellation(error))
            {
                _currentUser =
                    UserSession.Guest();


                EnterSignedOutState();
            }
            catch (Exception error)
            {
                _currentUser =
                    UserSession.Guest();


                EnterSignedOutState();


                MessageBox.Show(
                    error.Message,
                    "Sign-in Error",
                    MessageBoxButton.OK,
                    MessageBoxImage.Warning
                );
            }
            finally
            {
                _signInInProgress =
                    false;


                _signedOutView?.SetLoading(
                    false
                );
            }
        }


        private async void SignInMenuItem_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            await SignInAsync();
        }


        private IntPtr GetWindowHandle()
        {
            return new WindowInteropHelper(
                this
            ).Handle;
        }


        private static bool IsAuthenticationCancellation(
            MsalClientException error)
        {
            return
                string.Equals(
                    error.ErrorCode,
                    "authentication_canceled",
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                )
                ||
                string.Equals(
                    error.ErrorCode,
                    "wam_account_picker_canceled",
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                )
                ||
                string.Equals(
                    error.ErrorCode,
                    "user_canceled",
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // SIGN OUT
        // ==========================================

        private async void SignOutMenuItem_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            // Hide authenticated content immediately.

            CancelMarketRefresh();


            _currentUser =
                UserSession.Guest();


            EnterSignedOutState();


            try
            {
                await _authenticationService
                    .SignOutAsync();


                await LoadCurrentUserPreferencesAsync();


                UpdateUserInterface();
            }
            catch (Exception error)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    error.Message,
                    "Sign-out Error",
                    MessageBoxButton.OK,
                    MessageBoxImage.Warning
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // MARKET METADATA
        // ==========================================

        private async Task LoadMetadataAsync()
        {
            if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
            {
                EnterSignedOutState();

                return;
            }


            ShowLoadingStatus(
                "Loading available markets..."
            );


            try
            {
                MarketMetadataResult result =
                    await _marketDataService
                        .GetMetadataAsync();


                if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
                {
                    return;
                }


                MarketDataStore.Instance.SetMetadata(
                    result
                );


                if (!result.Success)
                {
                    ShowErrorStatus(
                        result.Error
                        ??
                        "Market metadata could not be loaded."
                    );

                    return;
                }


                KrakenMarket? selectedMarket =
                    SelectInitialMarket(
                        result.Markets
                    );


                MarketPeriod? selectedPeriod =
                    SelectInitialPeriod(
                        result.Periods
                    );


                if (
                    selectedMarket is null
                    ||
                    selectedPeriod is null
                )
                {
                    ShowErrorStatus(
                        "No usable market configuration is available."
                    );

                    return;
                }


                _preferences.MarketPair =
                    selectedMarket.Pair;

                _preferences.QuoteCurrency =
                    selectedMarket.QuoteAsset;

                _preferences.PeriodDays =
                    selectedPeriod.Days;

                _preferences.IntervalMinutes =
                    DailyInterval;


                await SaveCurrentUserPreferencesAsync();


                await ApplySelectionAndRefreshAsync(
                    selectedMarket.Pair,
                    selectedPeriod.Days
                );
            }
            catch (Exception error)
            {
                if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
                {
                    return;
                }


                MarketDataStore.Instance.SetError(
                    error.Message
                );


                ShowErrorStatus(
                    error.Message
                );
            }
        }


        private KrakenMarket? SelectInitialMarket(
            IReadOnlyList<KrakenMarket> markets)
        {
            if (markets.Count == 0)
            {
                return null;
            }


            string quoteCurrency =
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    _preferences.QuoteCurrency
                )
                    ? DefaultQuoteCurrency
                    : _preferences
                        .QuoteCurrency
                        .Trim()
                        .ToUpperInvariant();


            List<KrakenMarket> marketsForCurrency =
                markets
                    .Where(
                        market =>
                            string.Equals(
                                market.QuoteAsset,
                                quoteCurrency,
                                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                            )
                    )
                    .ToList();


            KrakenMarket? selectedMarket =
                marketsForCurrency.FirstOrDefault(
                    market =>
                        string.Equals(
                            market.Pair,
                            _preferences.MarketPair,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                );


            selectedMarket ??=
                marketsForCurrency.FirstOrDefault(
                    market =>
                        string.Equals(
                            market.BaseAsset,
                            DefaultBaseAsset,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                );


            selectedMarket ??=
                marketsForCurrency.FirstOrDefault();


            selectedMarket ??=
                markets.FirstOrDefault(
                    market =>
                        string.Equals(
                            market.BaseAsset,
                            DefaultBaseAsset,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                        &&
                        string.Equals(
                            market.QuoteAsset,
                            DefaultQuoteCurrency,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                );


            selectedMarket ??=
                markets.FirstOrDefault();


            return selectedMarket;
        }


        private MarketPeriod? SelectInitialPeriod(
            IReadOnlyList<MarketPeriod> periods)
        {
            if (periods.Count == 0)
            {
                return null;
            }


            MarketPeriod? selectedPeriod =
                null;


            if (_preferences.PeriodDays.HasValue)
            {
                selectedPeriod =
                    periods.FirstOrDefault(
                        period =>
                            period.Days ==
                            _preferences.PeriodDays.Value
                    );
            }


            selectedPeriod ??=
                periods.FirstOrDefault(
                    period =>
                        period.IsDefault
                );


            selectedPeriod ??=
                periods.FirstOrDefault(
                    period =>
                        period.Days ==
                        DefaultPeriodDays
                );


            selectedPeriod ??=
                periods.FirstOrDefault();


            return selectedPeriod;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // APPLY MARKET SELECTION
        // ==========================================

        private async Task ApplySelectionAndRefreshAsync(
            string pair,
            int periodDays)
        {
            if (
                !_currentUser.IsAuthenticated
                ||
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    pair
                )
            )
            {
                return;
            }


            int validPeriodDays =
                periodDays > 0
                    ? periodDays
                    : DefaultPeriodDays;


            MarketDataStore.Instance.SetSelection(
                pair.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(),
                DailyInterval,
                validPeriodDays
            );


            await RefreshMarketDataAsync();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // REFRESH MARKET DATA
        // ==========================================

        private async Task RefreshMarketDataAsync()
        {
            if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
            {
                return;
            }


            MarketDataStore store =
                MarketDataStore.Instance;


            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    store.Pair
                )
            )
            {
                return;
            }


            CancelMarketRefresh();


            _refreshCancellation =
                new CancellationTokenSource();


            CancellationToken cancellationToken =
                _refreshCancellation.Token;


            string pair =
                store.Pair
                    .Trim()
                    .ToUpperInvariant();


            int selectedPeriodDays =
                store.PeriodDays > 0
                    ? store.PeriodDays
                    : DefaultPeriodDays;


            MarketDataStore.Instance.SetLoading();


            ShowLoadingStatus(
                $"Loading {GetMarketDisplayName(pair)}..."
            );


            try
            {
                MarketDataResult rawResult =
                    await _marketDataService
                        .GetMarketDataAsync(
                            pair,
                            DailyInterval,
                            cancellationToken
                        );


                if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
                {
                    return;
                }


                if (!rawResult.Success)
                {
                    MarketDataStore.Instance.SetResult(
                        rawResult
                    );


                    ShowErrorStatus(
                        rawResult.Error
                        ??
                        "Market data could not be loaded."
                    );

                    return;
                }


                List<MarketCandle> filteredCandles =
                    FilterCandlesByPeriod(
                        rawResult.Candles,
                        selectedPeriodDays
                    );


                var filteredResult =
                    new MarketDataResult
                    {
                        Success =
                            true,

                        Error =
                            null,

                        Pair =
                            rawResult.Pair,

                        Interval =
                            rawResult.Interval,

                        Candles =
                            filteredCandles
                    };


                MarketDataStore.Instance.SetResult(
                    filteredResult
                );


                ShowSuccessStatus(
                    filteredResult.Pair,
                    filteredResult.Candles.Count
                );
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException)
            {
                // A newer request or sign-out
                // cancelled this request.
            }
            catch (Exception error)
            {
                if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
                {
                    return;
                }


                MarketDataStore.Instance.SetError(
                    error.Message
                );


                ShowErrorStatus(
                    error.Message
                );
            }
        }


        private static List<MarketCandle>
            FilterCandlesByPeriod(
                IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle> candles,
                int periodDays)
        {
            if (candles.Count == 0)
            {
                return new List<MarketCandle>();
            }


            List<MarketCandle> orderedCandles =
                candles
                    .OrderBy(
                        candle =>
                            candle.Date
                    )
                    .ToList();


            if (periodDays <= 0)
            {
                return orderedCandles;
            }


            DateTimeOffset latestDate =
                orderedCandles[^1].Date;


            DateTimeOffset earliestDate =
                latestDate.AddDays(
                    -periodDays
                );


            return orderedCandles
                .Where(
                    candle =>
                        candle.Date >=
                        earliestDate
                )
                .ToList();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // EXCEL / API SWITCH
        // ==========================================

        private async void UseApiDataButton_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
            {
                EnterSignedOutState();

                return;
            }


            CancelMarketRefresh();


            MarketDataStore.Instance
                .ClearImportedData();


            await LoadMetadataAsync();


            ShowDashboard();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // STORE CHANGED
        // ==========================================

        private void MarketDataStore_Changed(
            object? sender,
            EventArgs e)
        {
            Dispatcher.Invoke(
                UpdateGlobalDataSource
            );
        }


        private void UpdateGlobalDataSource()
        {
            if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
            {
                DataSourceText.Text =
                    "—";

                UseApiDataButton.Visibility =
                    Visibility.Collapsed;

                return;
            }


            MarketDataStore store =
                MarketDataStore.Instance;


            DataSourceText.Text =
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    store.DataSource
                )
                    ? "—"
                    : store.DataSource;


            bool isExcel =
                (
                    store.DataSource
                    ??
                    ""
                ).StartsWith(
                    "Excel",
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                );


            UseApiDataButton.Visibility =
                isExcel
                    ? Visibility.Visible
                    : Visibility.Collapsed;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // NAVIGATION STATE
        // ==========================================

        {
            UseApiDataButton.IsEnabled =
                _currentUser.IsAuthenticated;
        }

            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            ShowChart();
        }


        private void ShowChart()
        {
            if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
            {
                ShowSignedOutView();

                return;
            }


            var chartView =
                new ChartView(
                    _preferences.QuoteCurrency
                );


            chartView.ChartPreferencesChanged +=
                ChartPreferencesChanged;


            MainContent.Content =
                chartView;
        }


        private async void ChartPreferencesChanged(
            object? sender,
            ChartPreferencesChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
            {
                EnterSignedOutState();

                return;
            }


            MarketDataStore store =
                MarketDataStore.Instance;


            KrakenMarket? selectedMarket =
                store.Markets.FirstOrDefault(
                    market =>
                        string.Equals(
                            market.Pair,
                            e.Pair,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                        &&
                        string.Equals(
                            market.QuoteAsset,
                            _preferences.QuoteCurrency,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                );


            if (selectedMarket is null)
            {
                ShowErrorStatus(
                    "The selected market is unavailable."
                );

                return;
            }


            int periodDays =
                e.PeriodDays > 0
                    ? e.PeriodDays
                    : DefaultPeriodDays;


            _preferences.MarketPair =
                selectedMarket.Pair;

            _preferences.QuoteCurrency =
                selectedMarket.QuoteAsset;

            _preferences.PeriodDays =
                periodDays;


            await SaveCurrentUserPreferencesAsync();


            await ApplySelectionAndRefreshAsync(
                selectedMarket.Pair,
                periodDays
            );
        }

                $"{store.Candles.Count:N0} imported observations";


            DataStatusIndicator.Fill =
                FindBrush(
                    "PositiveBrush"
                );


            ShowChart();
        }

        private void SettingsMenuItem_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
            {
                ShowSignedOutView();

                return;
            }


            var settingsView =
                new SettingsView(
                    _preferences,
                    _preferencesService,
                    CurrentPreferenceAccountId
                );


            settingsView.DataPreferencesChanged +=
                SettingsView_DataPreferencesChanged;


            settingsView.GeneralPreferencesChanged +=
                SettingsView_GeneralPreferencesChanged;


            MainContent.Content =
                settingsView;
        }


        private async void SettingsView_DataPreferencesChanged(
            object? sender,
            DataPreferencesChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
            {
                EnterSignedOutState();

                return;
            }


            MarketDataStore store =
                MarketDataStore.Instance;


            string requestedQuoteCurrency =
                e.QuoteCurrency
                    .Trim()
                    .ToUpperInvariant();


            KrakenMarket? currentMarket =
                store.Markets.FirstOrDefault(
                    market =>
                        string.Equals(
                            market.Pair,
                            store.Pair,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                );


            KrakenMarket? selectedMarket =
                null;


            if (currentMarket is not null)
            {
                selectedMarket =
                    store.Markets.FirstOrDefault(
                        market =>
                            string.Equals(
                                market.BaseAsset,
                                currentMarket.BaseAsset,
                                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                            )
                            &&
                            string.Equals(
                                market.QuoteAsset,
                                requestedQuoteCurrency,
                                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                            )
                    );
            }


            selectedMarket ??=
                store.Markets.FirstOrDefault(
                    market =>
                        string.Equals(
                            market.BaseAsset,
                            DefaultBaseAsset,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                        &&
                        string.Equals(
                            market.QuoteAsset,
                            requestedQuoteCurrency,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                );


            selectedMarket ??=
                store.Markets.FirstOrDefault(
                    market =>
                        string.Equals(
                            market.QuoteAsset,
                            requestedQuoteCurrency,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                );


            if (selectedMarket is null)
            {
                ShowErrorStatus(
                    "No market is available for the selected currency."
                );

                return;
            }


            int periodDays =
                store.PeriodDays > 0
                    ? store.PeriodDays
                    : DefaultPeriodDays;


            _preferences.QuoteCurrency =
                selectedMarket.QuoteAsset;

            _preferences.MarketPair =
                selectedMarket.Pair;

            _preferences.PeriodDays =
                periodDays;


            await SaveCurrentUserPreferencesAsync();


            await ApplySelectionAndRefreshAsync(
                selectedMarket.Pair,
                periodDays
            );
        }


        private async void SettingsView_GeneralPreferencesChanged(
            object? sender,
            GeneralPreferencesChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            _preferences.Language =
                e.Language;

            _preferences.Theme =
                e.Theme;


            _themeService.ApplyTheme(
                e.Theme
            );


            await SaveCurrentUserPreferencesAsync();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // USER INTERFACE
        // ==========================================

        private void UpdateUserInterface()
        {
            if (_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
            {
                UserMenuItem.Header =
                    !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                        _currentUser.DisplayName
                    )
                        ? _currentUser.DisplayName
                        : !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                            _currentUser.Username
                        )
                            ? _currentUser.Username
                            : "Microsoft User";


                SignInMenuItem.Visibility =
                    Visibility.Collapsed;


                SignOutMenuItem.Visibility =
                    Visibility.Visible;
            }
            else
            {
                UserMenuItem.Header =
                    "Guest";


                SignInMenuItem.Visibility =
                    Visibility.Visible;


                SignOutMenuItem.Visibility =
                    Visibility.Collapsed;
            }


            UpdateAuthenticatedNavigation();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // STATUS
        // ==========================================

        private string GetMarketDisplayName(
            string pair)
        {
            KrakenMarket? market =
                MarketDataStore.Instance
                    .Markets
                    .FirstOrDefault(
                        item =>
                            string.Equals(
                                item.Pair,
                                pair,
                                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                            )
                    );


            return
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    market?.DisplayName
                )
                    ? market.DisplayName
                    : pair;
        }


        private void ShowLoadingStatus(
            string message)
        {
            DataStatusText.Text =
                message;


            DataStatusIndicator.Fill =
                FindBrush(
                    "WarningBrush"
                );
        }


        private void ShowSuccessStatus(
            string pair,
            int count)
        {
            DataStatusText.Text =
                $"{GetMarketDisplayName(pair)} · " +
                $"{count:N0} daily observations";


            DataStatusIndicator.Fill =
                FindBrush(
                    "PositiveBrush"
                );
        }


        private void ShowErrorStatus(
            string error)
        {
            DataStatusText.Text =
                "Market data unavailable";


            DataStatusIndicator.Fill =
                FindBrush(
                    "NegativeBrush"
                );


            MessageBox.Show(
                error,
                "Market Data Error",
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Warning
            );
        }


        private Brush FindBrush(
            string resourceName)
        {
            return (Brush)FindResource(
                resourceName
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // ABOUT
        // ==========================================

        private void AboutMenuItem_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                "Crypto Statistics\n" +
                "Quantitative cryptocurrency analysis.",
                "About",
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Information
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CLOSE AND SIGN OUT
        // ==========================================

        private async void MainWindow_Closing(
            object? sender,
            CancelEventArgs e)
        {
            if (_allowClose)
            {
                return;
            }


            e.Cancel =
                true;


            CancelMarketRefresh();


            try
            {
                if (_currentUser.IsAuthenticated)
                {
                    await _authenticationService
                        .SignOutAsync();
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                // Shutdown should continue even if
                // token-cache cleanup fails.
            }


            _currentUser =
                UserSession.Guest();


            _allowClose =
                true;


            Close();
        }


        protected override void OnClosed(
            EventArgs e)
        {
            MarketDataStore.Instance.Changed -=
                MarketDataStore_Changed;


            CancelMarketRefresh();


            _marketDataService.Dispose();


            base.OnClosed(e);
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace EthStatistics
{
    internal static unsafe class NativeMethods
    {
        // ==========================================
        // DLL
        // ==========================================

        private const string QuantEngineDll =
            "QuantEngine.dll";


        // ==========================================
        // BASIC MATHEMATICS
        // ==========================================

        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "Add",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        private static extern double Add(
            double a,
            double b
        );


        // ==========================================
        // MEAN ESTIMATORS
        // ==========================================

        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "Mean",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        private static extern double Mean(
            double* values,
            int size
        );


        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "GeometricMean",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        private static extern double GeometricMean(
            double* values,
            int size
        );


        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "HarmonicMean",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        private static extern double HarmonicMean(
            double* values,
            int size
        );


        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "WeightedMean",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        private static extern double WeightedMean(
            double* values,
            double* weights,
            int size
        );


        // ==========================================
        // DISPERSION
        // ==========================================

        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "Variance",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        private static extern double Variance(
            double* values,
            int size
        );


        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "StandardDeviation",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        private static extern double StandardDeviation(
            double* values,
            int size
        );


        // ==========================================
        // RETURNS
        // ==========================================

        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "LogReturn",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        internal static extern double LogReturn(
            double previousPrice,
            double currentPrice
        );


        // ==========================================
        // RSI
        // ==========================================

        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "WilderRSI",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        private static extern void WilderRSI(
            double* prices,
            int size,
            int period,
            double* output
        );


        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,
            EntryPoint = "CutlerRSI",
            CallingConvention = CallingConvention.Cdecl,
            ExactSpelling = true
        )]
        private static extern void CutlerRSI(
            double* prices,
            int size,
            int period,
            double* output
        );


        [DllImport(
            QuantEngineDll,

        )]
        private static extern void ExponentialRSI(
            double* prices,

            double* output
