        // ==========================================
        // ADD WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double CalculateAdd(
            double a,
            double b)
        {
            return Add(
                a,
                b
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // ARITHMETIC MEAN WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double CalculateMean(
            double[] values)
        {
            ValidateValues(
                values,
                1
            );

            fixed (double* valuesPointer = values)
            {
                return Mean(
                    valuesPointer,
                    values.Length
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // GEOMETRIC MEAN WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double CalculateGeometricMean(
            double[] values)
        {
            ValidatePositiveValues(
                values,
                1
            );

            fixed (double* valuesPointer = values)
            {
                return GeometricMean(
                    valuesPointer,
                    values.Length
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // HARMONIC MEAN WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double CalculateHarmonicMean(
            double[] values)
        {
            ValidatePositiveValues(
                values,
                1
            );

            fixed (double* valuesPointer = values)
            {
                return HarmonicMean(
                    valuesPointer,
                    values.Length
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // WEIGHTED MEAN WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double CalculateWeightedMean(
            double[] values,
            double[] weights)
        {
            ValidateValues(
                values,
                1
            );

            ValidateValues(
                weights,
                1
            );

            if (
                values.Length !=
                weights.Length
            )
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "Values and weights must have the same number of observations."
                );
            }

            bool hasPositiveWeight =
                false;

            for (
                int index = 0;
                index < weights.Length;
                index++
            )
            {
                if (weights[index] < 0.0)
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "Weights cannot be negative.",
                        nameof(weights)
                    );
                }

                if (weights[index] > 0.0)
                {
                    hasPositiveWeight =
                        true;
                }
            }

            if (!hasPositiveWeight)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "At least one weight must be positive.",
                    nameof(weights)
                );
            }

            fixed (double* valuesPointer = values)
            fixed (double* weightsPointer = weights)
            {
                return WeightedMean(
                    valuesPointer,
                    weightsPointer,
                    values.Length
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // VARIANCE WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double CalculateVariance(
            double[] values)
        {
            ValidateValues(
                values,
                2
            );

            fixed (double* valuesPointer = values)
            {
                return Variance(
                    valuesPointer,
                    values.Length
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // STANDARD DEVIATION WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double CalculateStandardDeviation(
            double[] values)
        {
            ValidateValues(
                values,
                2
            );

            fixed (double* valuesPointer = values)
            {
                return StandardDeviation(
                    valuesPointer,
                    values.Length
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // WILDER RSI WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double[] CalculateWilderRsi(
            double[] prices,
            int period)
        {
            ValidateRsiInput(
                prices,
                period
            );

            double[] output =
                CreateOutputArray(
                    prices.Length
                );

            fixed (double* pricesPointer = prices)
            fixed (double* outputPointer = output)
            {
                WilderRSI(
                    pricesPointer,
                    prices.Length,
                    period,
                    outputPointer
                );
            }

            return output;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CUTLER RSI WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double[] CalculateCutlerRsi(
            double[] prices,
            int period)
        {
            ValidateRsiInput(
                prices,
                period
            );

            double[] output =
                CreateOutputArray(
                    prices.Length
                );

            fixed (double* pricesPointer = prices)
            fixed (double* outputPointer = output)
            {
                CutlerRSI(
                    pricesPointer,
                    prices.Length,
                    period,
                    outputPointer
                );
            }

            return output;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // EXPONENTIAL RSI WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double[] CalculateExponentialRsi(
            double[] prices,
            int period)
        {
            ValidateRsiInput(
                prices,
                period
            );

            double[] output =
                CreateOutputArray(
                    prices.Length
                );

            fixed (double* pricesPointer = prices)
            fixed (double* outputPointer = output)
            {
                ExponentialRSI(
                    pricesPointer,
                    prices.Length,
                    period,
                    outputPointer
                );
            }

            return output;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // STOCHASTIC RSI WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double[] CalculateStochasticRsi(
            double[] prices,
            int rsiPeriod,
            int stochasticPeriod)
        {
            ValidateRsiInput(
                prices,
                rsiPeriod
            );

            if (stochasticPeriod <= 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(stochasticPeriod),
                    "Stochastic period must be greater than zero."
                );
            }

            double[] output =
                CreateOutputArray(
                    prices.Length
                );

            fixed (double* pricesPointer = prices)
            fixed (double* outputPointer = output)
            {
                StochasticRSI(
                    pricesPointer,
                    prices.Length,
                    rsiPeriod,
                    stochasticPeriod,
                    outputPointer
                );
            }

            return output;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // PRICE STREAK WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double[] CalculatePriceStreak(
            double[] prices)
        {
            ValidatePositiveValues(
                prices,
                1
            );

            double[] output =
                CreateOutputArray(
                    prices.Length
                );

            fixed (double* pricesPointer = prices)
            fixed (double* outputPointer = output)
            {
                PriceStreak(
                    pricesPointer,
                    prices.Length,
                    outputPointer
                );
            }

            return output;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // PERCENT RANK WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double[] CalculatePercentRank(
            double[] values,
            int period)
        {
            ValidateValues(
                values,
                1
            );

            if (period <= 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(period),
                    "Period must be greater than zero."
                );
            }

            if (values.Length <= period)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "The values array must contain more observations than the period.",
                    nameof(values)
                );
            }

            double[] output =
                CreateOutputArray(
                    values.Length
                );

            fixed (double* valuesPointer = values)
            fixed (double* outputPointer = output)
            {
                PercentRank(
                    valuesPointer,
                    values.Length,
                    period,
                    outputPointer
                );
            }

            return output;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CONNORS RSI WRAPPER
        // ==========================================

        internal static double[] CalculateConnorsRsi(
            double[] prices,
            int priceRsiPeriod,
            int streakRsiPeriod,
            int rankPeriod)
        {
            ValidatePositiveValues(
                prices,
                1
            );

            if (priceRsiPeriod <= 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(priceRsiPeriod)
                );
            }

            if (streakRsiPeriod <= 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(streakRsiPeriod)
                );
            }

            if (rankPeriod <= 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(rankPeriod)
                );
            }

            double[] output =
                CreateOutputArray(
                    prices.Length
                );

            fixed (double* pricesPointer = prices)
            fixed (double* outputPointer = output)
            {
                ConnorsRSI(
                    pricesPointer,
                    prices.Length,
                    priceRsiPeriod,
                    streakRsiPeriod,
                    rankPeriod,
                    outputPointer
                );
            }

            return output;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // VALIDATION
        // ==========================================

        private static void ValidateValues(
            double[] values,
            int minimumSize)
        {
            ArgumentNullException.ThrowIfNull(
                values
            );

            if (values.Length < minimumSize)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    $"At least {minimumSize} values are required.",
                    nameof(values)
                );
            }

            for (
                int index = 0;
                index < values.Length;
                index++
            )
            {
                if (!double.IsFinite(values[index]))
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "All values must be finite.",
                        nameof(values)
                    );
                }
            }
        }


        private static void ValidatePositiveValues(
            double[] values,
            int minimumSize)
        {
            ValidateValues(
                values,
                minimumSize
            );

            for (
                int index = 0;
                index < values.Length;
                index++
            )
            {
                if (values[index] <= 0.0)
                {
                    throw new ArgumentException(
                        "All values must be greater than zero.",
                        nameof(values)
                    );
                }
            }
        }


        private static void ValidateRsiInput(
            double[] prices,
            int period)
        {
            ValidatePositiveValues(
                prices,
                1
            );

            if (period <= 0)
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(period),
                    "RSI period must be greater than zero."
                );
            }

            if (prices.Length <= period)
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "The price array must contain more observations than the RSI period.",
                    nameof(prices)
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // OUTPUT
        // ==========================================

        private static double[] CreateOutputArray(
            int size)
        {
            double[] output =
                new double[size];

            Array.Fill(
                output,
                double.NaN
            );

            return output;
        }
    }
}

namespace EthStatistics.Models
{
    public sealed class KrakenMarket
    {
        public string Pair { get; init; } =
            "";

        public string DisplayName { get; init; } =
            "";

        public string BaseAsset { get; init; } =
            "";

        public string QuoteAsset { get; init; } =
            "";


        public override string ToString()
        {
            if (
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    DisplayName
                )
            )
            {
                return DisplayName;
            }


            if (
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    BaseAsset
                )
                &&
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    QuoteAsset
                )
            )
            {
                return
                    $"{BaseAsset}/{QuoteAsset}";
            }


            return Pair;
        }
    }
}

namespace EthStatistics.Models
{
    public sealed class MarketPeriod
    {
        public string Name { get; init; } =
            "";

        public int Days { get; init; }

        public bool IsDefault { get; init; }


        public override string ToString()
        {
            if (
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    Name
                )
            )
            {
                return Name;
            }


            return Days switch
            {
                30 =>
                    "30 Days",

                90 =>
                    "90 Days",

                180 =>
                    "180 Days",

                365 =>
                    "1 Year",

                720 =>
                    "Maximum",

                _ =>
                    $"{Days} Days"
            };
        }
    }
}

============================================================
FILE: EthStatistics\Services\AuthenticationService.cs
============================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using EthStatistics.Models;
using Microsoft.Identity.Client;
using Microsoft.Identity.Client.Broker;

namespace EthStatistics.Services
{
    public sealed class AuthenticationService
    {
        private const string ClientId =
      "a5fb1e23-605f-4228-98a7-be4eb2a9bb7d";

        private const string Authority =
            "https://login.microsoftonline.com/common";

        private static readonly string[] Scopes =
        [
            "User.Read"
        ];


      

        private readonly IPublicClientApplication
            _application;


        public AuthenticationService()
        {
            var brokerOptions =
                new BrokerOptions(
                    BrokerOptions.OperatingSystems.Windows
                )
                {
                    Title = "EthStatistics"
                };

            _application =
                PublicClientApplicationBuilder
                    .Create(ClientId)
                    .WithAuthority(Authority)
                    .WithBroker(brokerOptions)
                    .Build();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // SILENT SIGN-IN
        // ==========================================

        public async Task<UserSession?>
            TrySignInSilentlyAsync()
        {
            IAccount? account =
                (
                    await _application
                        .GetAccountsAsync()
                )
                .FirstOrDefault();

            account ??=
                PublicClientApplication
                    .OperatingSystemAccount;

            try
            {
                AuthenticationResult result =
                    await _application
                        .AcquireTokenSilent(
                            Scopes,
                            account
                        )
                        .ExecuteAsync();

                return CreateSession(result);
            }
            catch (MsalUiRequiredException)
            {
                return null;
            }
            catch (MsalException)
            {
                return null;
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // INTERACTIVE WAM SIGN-IN
        // ==========================================

        public async Task<UserSession> SignInAsync(
            IntPtr parentWindowHandle)
        {
            IAccount? account =
                (
                    await _application
                        .GetAccountsAsync()
                )
                .FirstOrDefault();

            account ??=
                PublicClientApplication
                    .OperatingSystemAccount;

            try
            {
                AuthenticationResult silentResult =
                    await _application
                        .AcquireTokenSilent(
                            Scopes,
                            account
                        )
                        .ExecuteAsync();

                return CreateSession(
                    silentResult
                );
            }
            catch (MsalUiRequiredException)
            {
                // Interactive WAM sign-in is required.
            }

            AuthenticationResult interactiveResult =
                await _application
                    .AcquireTokenInteractive(
                        Scopes
                    )
                    .WithAccount(account)
                    .WithParentActivityOrWindow(
                        parentWindowHandle
                    )
                    .WithPrompt(
                        Prompt.SelectAccount
                    )
                    .ExecuteAsync();

            return CreateSession(
                interactiveResult
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // SIGN OUT
        // ==========================================

        public async Task SignOutAsync()
        {
            IEnumerable<IAccount> accounts =
                await _application
                    .GetAccountsAsync();

            foreach (IAccount account in accounts)
            {
                await _application.RemoveAsync(
                    account
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CREATE APPLICATION SESSION
        // ==========================================

        private static UserSession CreateSession(
            AuthenticationResult result)
        {
            string username =
                result.Account?.Username
                ?? GetClaim(
                    result.ClaimsPrincipal,
                    "preferred_username"
                )
                ?? GetClaim(
                    result.ClaimsPrincipal,
                    ClaimTypes.Email
                )
                ?? "";

            string displayName =
                GetClaim(
                    result.ClaimsPrincipal,
                    "name"
                )
                ?? GetClaim(
                    result.ClaimsPrincipal,
                    ClaimTypes.Name
                )
                ?? username;

            string accountId =
                result.Account?
                    .HomeAccountId?
                    .Identifier
                ?? GetClaim(
                    result.ClaimsPrincipal,
                    "sub"
                )
                ?? "";

            return new UserSession
            {
                AccountId =
                    accountId,

                Username =
                    username,

                DisplayName =
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                        displayName
                    )
                        ? "Microsoft User"
                        : displayName,

                IsAuthenticated =
                    true
            };
        }


        private static string? GetClaim(
            ClaimsPrincipal? claimsPrincipal,
            string claimType)
        {
            return claimsPrincipal?
                .Claims
                .FirstOrDefault(
                    claim =>
                        string.Equals(
                            claim.Type,
                            claimType,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                )?
                .Value;
        }
    }
}

============================================================
FILE: EthStatistics\Services\ExcelImportService.cs
============================================================
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using ExcelDataReader;
using EthStatistics.Models;

namespace EthStatistics.Services
{
    public sealed class ExcelImportService
    {
        public ExcelImportService()
        {
            Encoding.RegisterProvider(
                CodePagesEncodingProvider.Instance
            );
        }


        public IReadOnlyList<ExcelSheetInfo> ReadWorkbook(
            string filePath)
        {
            if (!File.Exists(filePath))
            {
                throw new FileNotFoundException(
                    "The selected Excel file does not exist.",
                    filePath
                );
            }

            using FileStream stream =
                File.Open(
                    filePath,
                    FileMode.Open,
                    FileAccess.Read,
                    FileShare.ReadWrite
                );

            using IExcelDataReader reader =
                ExcelReaderFactory.CreateReader(stream);

            var sheets =
                new List<ExcelSheetInfo>();

            do
            {
                ExcelSheetInfo? sheet =
                    ReadCurrentSheet(reader);

                if (sheet is not null)
                {
                    sheets.Add(sheet);
                }
            }
            while (reader.NextResult());

            return sheets;
        }


        private static ExcelSheetInfo? ReadCurrentSheet(
            IExcelDataReader reader)
        {
            if (!reader.Read())
            {
                return null;
            }

            var columns = new List<string>();

            for (
                int columnIndex = 0;
                columnIndex < reader.FieldCount;
                columnIndex++)
            {
                string header =
                    reader.GetValue(columnIndex)?
                        .ToString()?
                        .Trim()
                    ?? "";

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(header))
                {
                    header =
                        $"Column {columnIndex + 1}";
                }

                header =
                    MakeUniqueHeader(
                        header,
                        columns
                    );

                columns.Add(header);
            }

            var rows =
                new List<ExcelImportRow>();

            while (reader.Read())
            {
                var values =
                    new Dictionary<string, ExcelCellValue>();

                bool hasAnyValue = false;

                for (
                    int columnIndex = 0;
                    columnIndex < columns.Count;
                    columnIndex++)
                {
                    object? value =
                        reader.GetValue(columnIndex);

                    if (
                        value is not null &&
                        !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                            value.ToString())
                    )
                    {
                        hasAnyValue = true;
                    }

                    string? numberFormat = null;

                    try
                    {
                        numberFormat =
                            reader.GetNumberFormatString(
                                columnIndex
                            );
                    }
                    catch
                    {
                        // Formatting metadata is optional.
                    }

                    values[columns[columnIndex]] =
                        new ExcelCellValue
                        {
                            Value = value,
                            NumberFormat = numberFormat
                        };
                }

                if (hasAnyValue)
                {
                    rows.Add(
                        new ExcelImportRow
                        {
                            Values = values
                        }
                    );
                }
            }

            return new ExcelSheetInfo
            {
                Name = reader.Name,
                Columns = columns,
                Rows = rows
            };
        }


        public ImportedValueType DetectValueType(
            ExcelSheetInfo sheet,
            string valueColumn)
        {
            int percentageFormatted = 0;
            int percentageStrings = 0;
            int usableValues = 0;

            foreach (ExcelImportRow row in sheet.Rows)
            {
                if (
                    !row.Values.TryGetValue(
                        valueColumn,
                        out ExcelCellValue? cell) ||
                    cell.Value is null
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }

                usableValues++;

                string text =
                    cell.Value.ToString()?.Trim() ?? "";

                if (text.EndsWith("%"))
                {
                    percentageStrings++;
                }

                if (
                    cell.NumberFormat?.Contains('%') == true
                )
                {
                    percentageFormatted++;
                }
            }

            if (usableValues == 0)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "The selected value column contains no usable values."
                );
            }

            return
                percentageFormatted > 0 ||
                percentageStrings > 0
                    ? ImportedValueType.PercentageReturn
                    : ImportedValueType.Price;
        }


        public ExcelImportResult Import(
            string filePath,
            ExcelSheetInfo sheet,
            string dateColumn,
            string valueColumn,
            ImportedValueType valueType)
        {
            var observations =
                new List<(DateTimeOffset Date, double Value)>();

            foreach (ExcelImportRow row in sheet.Rows)
            {
                if (
                    !row.Values.TryGetValue(
                        dateColumn,
                        out ExcelCellValue? dateCell) ||
                    !row.Values.TryGetValue(
                        valueColumn,
                        out ExcelCellValue? valueCell)
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (
                    !TryReadDate(
                        dateCell.Value,
                        out DateTimeOffset date)
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (
                    !TryReadNumber(
                        valueCell,
                        valueType,
                        out double value)
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }

                observations.Add(
                    (date, value)
                );
            }

            observations =
                observations
                    .OrderBy(item => item.Date)
                    .GroupBy(item => item.Date)
                    .Select(group => group.Last())
                    .ToList();

            if (observations.Count < 2)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "At least two valid date/value rows are required."
                );
            }

            IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle> candles;
            IReadOnlyList<double> importedReturns;

            if (
                valueType ==
                ImportedValueType.PercentageReturn
            )
            {
                importedReturns =
                    observations
                        .Select(item => item.Value)
                        .ToArray();

                candles =
                    BuildCandlesFromReturns(
                        observations
                    );
            }
            else
            {
                importedReturns =
                    Array.Empty<double>();

                candles =
                    BuildCandlesFromPrices(
                        observations
                    );
            }

            return new ExcelImportResult
            {
                FileName =
                    Path.GetFileName(filePath),

                SheetName =
                    sheet.Name,

                DateColumn =
                    dateColumn,

                ValueColumn =
                    valueColumn,

                ValueType =
                    valueType,

                Candles =
                    candles,

                ImportedReturns =
                    importedReturns,

                IntervalMinutes =
                    InferIntervalMinutes(
                        observations.Select(
                            item => item.Date
                        )
                    )
            };
        }


        private static IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle>
            BuildCandlesFromPrices(
                IReadOnlyList<(
                    DateTimeOffset Date,
                    double Value)> observations)
        {
            return observations
                .Where(item => item.Value > 0)
                .Select(
                    item => new MarketCandle
                    {
                        Date = item.Date,
                        Open = item.Value,
                        High = item.Value,
                        Low = item.Value,
                        Close = item.Value,
                        Vwap = item.Value,
                        Volume = 0,
                        TradeCount = 0
                    }
                )
                .ToArray();
        }


        private static IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle>
            BuildCandlesFromReturns(
                IReadOnlyList<(
                    DateTimeOffset Date,
                    double Value)> observations)
        {
            var candles =
                new List<MarketCandle>();

            double indexValue = 100.0;

            foreach (var observation in observations)
            {
                double previousValue =
                    indexValue;

                indexValue *=
                    1.0 + observation.Value;

                if (
                    !double.IsFinite(indexValue) ||
                    indexValue <= 0)
                {
                    throw new InvalidOperationException(
                        "The return series produces an invalid cumulative index."
                    );
                }

                candles.Add(
                    new MarketCandle
                    {
                        Date = observation.Date,
                        Open = previousValue,
                        High = Math.Max(
                            previousValue,
                            indexValue
                        ),
                        Low = Math.Min(
                            previousValue,
                            indexValue
                        ),
                        Close = indexValue,
                        Vwap = indexValue,
                        Volume = 0,
                        TradeCount = 0
                    }
                );
            }

            return candles;
        }


        private static bool TryReadDate(
            object? value,
            out DateTimeOffset result)
        {
            if (value is DateTime dateTime)
            {
                result =
                    new DateTimeOffset(dateTime);

                return true;
            }

            if (
                value is double excelSerial &&
                excelSerial > 0
            )
            {
                try
                {
                    DateTime converted =
                        DateTime.FromOADate(
                            excelSerial
                        );

                    result =
                        new DateTimeOffset(converted);

                    return true;
                }
                catch
                {
                }
            }

            string text =
                value?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? "";

            return DateTimeOffset.TryParse(
                text,
                CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
                DateTimeStyles.AssumeLocal,
                out result
            )
            ||
            DateTimeOffset.TryParse(
                text,
                CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                DateTimeStyles.AssumeUniversal,
                out result
            );
        }


        private static bool TryReadNumber(
            ExcelCellValue cell,
            ImportedValueType valueType,
            out double result)
        {
            result = 0;

            if (
                cell.Value is byte or
                short or
                int or
                long or
                float or
                double or
                decimal
            )
            {
                result =
                    Convert.ToDouble(
                        cell.Value,
                        CultureInfo.InvariantCulture
                    );

                return
                    double.IsFinite(result);
            }

            string text =
                cell.Value?
                    .ToString()?
                    .Trim()
                ?? "";

            bool containsPercent =
                text.EndsWith("%");

            text =
                text.Replace("%", "")
                    .Trim();

            bool parsed =
                double.TryParse(
                    text,
                    NumberStyles.Any,
                    CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
                    out result
                )
                ||
                double.TryParse(
                    text,
                    NumberStyles.Any,
                    CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    out result
                );

            if (!parsed)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (
                valueType ==
                    ImportedValueType.PercentageReturn &&
                containsPercent
            )
            {
                result /= 100.0;
            }

            return double.IsFinite(result);
        }


        private static int InferIntervalMinutes(
            IEnumerable<DateTimeOffset> dates)
        {
            DateTimeOffset[] sorted =
                dates
                    .OrderBy(date => date)
                    .ToArray();

            double[] differences =
                sorted
                    .Zip(
                        sorted.Skip(1),
                        (first, second) =>
                            (
                                second - first
                            ).TotalMinutes
                    )
                    .Where(value => value > 0)
                    .OrderBy(value => value)
                    .ToArray();

            if (differences.Length == 0)
            {
                return 1;
            }

            double median =
                differences[
                    differences.Length / 2
                ];

            return Math.Max(
                1,
                (int)Math.Round(median)
            );
        }


        private static string MakeUniqueHeader(
            string header,
            ICollection<string> existingHeaders)
        {
            if (!existingHeaders.Contains(header))
            {
                return header;
            }

            int suffix = 2;

            string candidate;

            do
            {
                candidate =
                    $"{header} ({suffix})";

                suffix++;
            }
            while (
                existingHeaders.Contains(candidate)
            );

            return candidate;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using EthStatistics.Models;

namespace EthStatistics.Services
{
    public sealed class KrakenMarketDataService
        : IDisposable
    {
        // ==========================================
        // CONSTANTS
        // ==========================================

        private const string AssetPairsEndpoint =
            "0/public/AssetPairs";

        private const string OhlcEndpoint =
            "0/public/OHLC";

        private const int DailyInterval =
            1440;


        private static readonly HashSet<int>
            ValidIntervals =
            new()
            {
                1,
                5,
                15,
                30,
                60,
                240,
                1440,
                10080,
                21600
            };


        private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<
            string,
            string
        > AssetAliases =
            new Dictionary<string, string>(
                StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase
            )
            {
                // Bitcoin

                ["XXBT"] =
                    "BTC",

                ["XBT"] =
                    "BTC",

                ["BTC"] =
                    "BTC",


                // Ethereum

                ["XETH"] =
                    "ETH",

                ["ETH"] =
                    "ETH",


                // Litecoin

                ["XLTC"] =
                    "LTC",

                ["LTC"] =
                    "LTC",


                // XRP

                ["XXRP"] =
                    "XRP",

                ["XRP"] =
                    "XRP",


                // Dogecoin

                ["XXDG"] =
                    "DOGE",

                ["XDG"] =
                    "DOGE",

                ["DOGE"] =
                    "DOGE",


                // Other Kraken legacy names

                ["XETC"] =
                    "ETC",

                ["ETC"] =
                    "ETC",

                ["XMLN"] =
                    "MLN",

                ["MLN"] =
                    "MLN",

                ["XREP"] =
                    "REP",

                ["REP"] =
                    "REP",


                // Fiat currencies

                ["ZUSD"] =
                    "USD",

                ["USD"] =
                    "USD",

                ["ZEUR"] =
                    "EUR",

                ["EUR"] =
                    "EUR",

                ["ZGBP"] =
                    "GBP",

                ["GBP"] =
                    "GBP",

                ["ZJPY"] =
                    "JPY",

                ["JPY"] =
                    "JPY",

                ["ZCAD"] =
                    "CAD",

                ["CAD"] =
                    "CAD",

                ["ZAUD"] =
                    "AUD",

                ["AUD"] =
                    "AUD",

                ["ZCHF"] =
                    "CHF",

                ["CHF"] =
                    "CHF"
            };


        // ==========================================
        // HTTP
        // ==========================================

        private readonly HttpClient
            _httpClient;

        private readonly bool
            _ownsHttpClient;


        // ==========================================
        // CACHE
        // ==========================================

        private MarketMetadataResult?
            _metadataCache;

        private readonly Dictionary<
            MarketCacheKey,
            MarketDataResult
        > _marketDataCache =
            new();


        private readonly SemaphoreSlim
            _metadataLock =
                new(
                    1,
                    1
                );

        private readonly SemaphoreSlim
            _marketDataLock =
                new(
                    1,
                    1
                );


        // ==========================================
        // CONSTRUCTORS
        // ==========================================

        public KrakenMarketDataService()
        {
            _httpClient =
                CreateHttpClient();

            _ownsHttpClient =
                true;
        }


        public KrakenMarketDataService(
            HttpClient httpClient)
        {
            _httpClient =
                httpClient
                ??
                throw new ArgumentNullException(
                    nameof(httpClient)
                );

            _ownsHttpClient =
                false;
        }


        private static HttpClient CreateHttpClient()
        {
            var httpClient =
                new HttpClient
                {
                    BaseAddress =
                        new Uri(
                            "https://api.kraken.com/"
                        ),

                    Timeout =
                        TimeSpan.FromSeconds(
                            30
                        )
                };


            httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders
                .UserAgent
                .ParseAdd(
                    "EthStatistics/1.0"
                );


            httpClient.DefaultRequestHeaders
                .Accept
                .ParseAdd(
                    "application/json"
                );


            return httpClient;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // METADATA
        // ==========================================

        public async Task<MarketMetadataResult>
            GetMetadataAsync(
                CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            if (_metadataCache is not null)
            {
                return _metadataCache;
            }


            await _metadataLock.WaitAsync(
                cancellationToken
            );


            try
            {
                if (_metadataCache is not null)
                {
                    return _metadataCache;
                }


                using JsonDocument document =
                    await GetJsonAsync(
                        AssetPairsEndpoint,
                        cancellationToken
                    );


                JsonElement root =
                    document.RootElement;


                ThrowIfKrakenReturnedErrors(
                    root
                );


                if (
                    !root.TryGetProperty(
                        "result",
                        out JsonElement resultElement
                    )
                    ||
                    resultElement.ValueKind !=
                    JsonValueKind.Object
                )
                {
                    return CreateMetadataError(
                        "Kraken returned no asset-pair metadata."
                    );
                }


                var uniqueMarkets =
                    new Dictionary<
                        string,
                        KrakenMarket
                    >(
                        StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                    );


                foreach (
                    JsonProperty pairProperty
                    in resultElement.EnumerateObject()
                )
                {
                    KrakenMarket? market =
                        ParseMarket(
                            pairProperty.Name,
                            pairProperty.Value
                        );


                    if (
                        market is null ||
                        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                            market.Pair
                        )
                    )
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    uniqueMarkets.TryAdd(
                        market.Pair,
                        market
                    );
                }


                List<KrakenMarket> markets =
                    uniqueMarkets.Values
                        .OrderBy(
                            market =>
                                market.QuoteAsset
                        )
                        .ThenBy(
                            market =>
                                market.BaseAsset
                        )
                        .ThenBy(
                            market =>
                                market.DisplayName
                        )
                        .ToList();


                if (markets.Count == 0)
                {
                    return CreateMetadataError(
                        "Kraken returned no usable online markets."
                    );
                }


                List<string> quoteCurrencies =
                    markets
                        .Select(
                            market =>
                                market.QuoteAsset
                        )
                        .Where(
                            value =>
                                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                    value
                                )
                        )
                        .Distinct(
                            StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                        .OrderBy(
                            value =>
                                value
                        )
                        .ToList();


                _metadataCache =
                    new MarketMetadataResult
                    {
                        Success =
                            true,

                        Error =
                            null,

                        Markets =
                            markets,

                        QuoteCurrencies =
                            quoteCurrencies,

                        Intervals =
                            CreateIntervals(),

                        Periods =
                            CreatePeriods()
                    };


                return _metadataCache;
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException)
            {
                throw;
            }
            catch (Exception error)
            {
                return CreateMetadataError(
                    GetFriendlyErrorMessage(
                        error
                    )
                );
            }
            finally
            {
                _metadataLock.Release();
            }
        }


        private static KrakenMarket? ParseMarket(
            string internalPair,
            JsonElement pairElement)
        {
            if (
                pairElement.ValueKind !=
                JsonValueKind.Object
            )
            {
                return null;
            }


            string status =
                GetString(
                    pairElement,
                    "status"
                );


            if (
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    status
                )
                &&
                !string.Equals(
                    status,
                    "online",
                    StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                )
            )
            {
                return null;
            }


            string restPair =
                GetString(
                    pairElement,
                    "altname"
                );


            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    restPair
                )
            )
            {
                restPair =
                    internalPair;
            }


            restPair =
                restPair
                    .Trim()
                    .ToUpperInvariant();


            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    restPair
                )
            )
            {
                return null;
            }


            string webSocketName =
                GetString(
                    pairElement,
                    "wsname"
                );


            string baseAsset;
            string quoteAsset;


            if (
                TrySplitDisplayPair(
                    webSocketName,
                    out string displayBase,
                    out string displayQuote
                )
            )
            {
                baseAsset =
                    NormalizeAsset(
                        displayBase
                    );

                quoteAsset =
                    NormalizeAsset(
                        displayQuote
                    );
            }
            else
            {
                baseAsset =
                    NormalizeAsset(
                        GetString(
                            pairElement,
                            "base"
                        )
                    );

                quoteAsset =
                    NormalizeAsset(
                        GetString(
                            pairElement,
                            "quote"
                        )
                    );
            }


            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    baseAsset
                )
                ||
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    quoteAsset
                )
            )
            {
                return null;
            }


            return new KrakenMarket
            {
                Pair =
                    restPair,

                DisplayName =
                    $"{baseAsset}/{quoteAsset}",

                BaseAsset =
                    baseAsset,

                QuoteAsset =
                    quoteAsset
            };
        }


        // ==========================================
        // MARKET DATA
        // ==========================================

        public async Task<MarketDataResult>
            GetMarketDataAsync(
                string pair,
                int interval,
                CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
        {
            string normalizedPair =
                ValidatePair(
                    pair
                );


            int normalizedInterval =
                ValidateInterval(
                    interval
                );


            var cacheKey =
                new MarketCacheKey(
                    normalizedPair,
                    normalizedInterval
                );


            if (
                _marketDataCache.TryGetValue(
                    cacheKey,
                    out MarketDataResult? cachedResult
                )
            )
            {
                return cachedResult;
            }


            await _marketDataLock.WaitAsync(
                cancellationToken
            );


            try
            {
                if (
                    _marketDataCache.TryGetValue(
                        cacheKey,
                        out cachedResult
                    )
                )
                {
                    return cachedResult;
                }


                string requestUri =
                    OhlcEndpoint +
                    "?pair=" +
                    Uri.EscapeDataString(
                        normalizedPair
                    )
                    +
                    "&interval=" +
                    normalizedInterval.ToString(
                        CultureInfo.InvariantCulture
                    );


                using JsonDocument document =
                    await GetJsonAsync(
                        requestUri,
                        cancellationToken
                    );


                JsonElement root =
                    document.RootElement;


                ThrowIfKrakenReturnedErrors(
                    root
                );


                if (
                    !root.TryGetProperty(
                        "result",
                        out JsonElement resultElement
                    )
                    ||
                    resultElement.ValueKind !=
                    JsonValueKind.Object
                )
                {
                    return CreateMarketDataError(
                        normalizedPair,
                        normalizedInterval,
                        "Kraken returned no OHLC result."
                    );
                }


                JsonElement candleArray =
                    default;


                bool foundSeries =
                    false;


                foreach (
                    JsonProperty property
                    in resultElement.EnumerateObject()
                )
                {
                    if (
                        string.Equals(
                            property.Name,
                            "last",
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                    )
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    if (
                        property.Value.ValueKind ==
                        JsonValueKind.Array
                    )
                    {
                        candleArray =
                            property.Value;

                        foundSeries =
                            true;

                        break;
                    }
                }


                if (!foundSeries)
                {
                    return CreateMarketDataError(
                        normalizedPair,
                        normalizedInterval,
                        "Kraken returned no OHLC series."
                    );
                }


                List<MarketCandle> candles =
                    ParseCandles(
                        candleArray
                    );


                // Kraken's last entry represents the
                // current, incomplete timeframe.

                if (candles.Count > 0)
                {
                    candles.RemoveAt(
                        candles.Count - 1
                    );
                }


                if (candles.Count == 0)
                {
                    return CreateMarketDataError(
                        normalizedPair,
                        normalizedInterval,
                        "Kraken returned no completed candles."
                    );
                }


                candles =
                    candles
                        .OrderBy(
                            candle =>
                                candle.Date
                        )
                        .ToList();


                var result =
                    new MarketDataResult
                    {
                        Success =
                            true,

                        Error =
                            null,

                        Pair =
                            normalizedPair,

                        Interval =
                            normalizedInterval,

                        Candles =
                            candles
                    };


                _marketDataCache[cacheKey] =
                    result;


                return result;
            }
            catch (OperationCanceledException)
            {
                throw;
            }
            catch (Exception error)
            {
                return CreateMarketDataError(
                    normalizedPair,
                    normalizedInterval,
                    GetFriendlyErrorMessage(
                        error
                    )
                );
            }
            finally
            {
                _marketDataLock.Release();
            }
        }


        private static List<MarketCandle> ParseCandles(
            JsonElement candleArray)
        {
            var candles =
                new List<MarketCandle>();


            foreach (
                JsonElement item
                in candleArray.EnumerateArray()
            )
            {
                if (
                    item.ValueKind !=
                    JsonValueKind.Array
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }


                JsonElement.ArrayEnumerator values =
                    item.EnumerateArray();


                var fields =
                    new List<JsonElement>(
                        8
                    );


                while (values.MoveNext())
                {
                    fields.Add(
                        values.Current
                    );
                }


                if (fields.Count < 8)
                {
                    continue;
                }


                long unixTimestamp =
                    ReadInt64(
                        fields[0]
                    );


                double open =
                    ReadDouble(
                        fields[1]
                    );


                double high =
                    ReadDouble(
                        fields[2]
                    );


                double low =
                    ReadDouble(
                        fields[3]
                    );


                double close =
                    ReadDouble(
                        fields[4]
                    );


                double vwap =
                    ReadDouble(
                        fields[5]
                    );


                double volume =
                    ReadDouble(
                        fields[6]
                    );


                int tradeCount =
     checked(
         (int)ReadInt64(
             fields[7]
         )
     
     );


                if (
                    !double.IsFinite(open) ||
                    !double.IsFinite(high) ||
                    !double.IsFinite(low) ||
                    !double.IsFinite(close) ||
                    !double.IsFinite(vwap) ||
                    !double.IsFinite(volume)
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }


                candles.Add(
                    new MarketCandle
                    {
                        Date =
                            DateTimeOffset
                                .FromUnixTimeSeconds(
                                    unixTimestamp
                                ),

                        Open =
                            open,

                        High =
                            high,

                        Low =
                            low,

                        Close =
                            close,

                        Vwap =
                            vwap,

                        Volume =
                            volume,

                        TradeCount =
                            tradeCount
                    }
                );
            }


            return candles;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CACHE MANAGEMENT
        // ==========================================

        public void InvalidateMarketDataCache(
            string? pair = null)
        {
            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    pair
                )
            )
            {
                _marketDataCache.Clear();

                return;
            }


            string normalizedPair =
                pair
                    .Trim()
                    .ToUpperInvariant();


            List<MarketCacheKey> keysToRemove =
                _marketDataCache.Keys
                    .Where(
                        key =>
                            string.Equals(
                                key.Pair,
                                normalizedPair,
                                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                            )
                    )
                    .ToList();


            foreach (
                MarketCacheKey key
                in keysToRemove
            )
            {
                _marketDataCache.Remove(
                    key
                );
            }
        }


        public void InvalidateMetadataCache()
        {
            _metadataCache =
                null;
        }

        // ==========================================

            [
                new MarketInterval

                    IsDefault =

                },

                new MarketInterval
                {
                    Name =
                        "15 Minutes",

                    Minutes =
                        15,

                    IsDefault =

                },

                new MarketInterval
                {
                    Name =
                        "30 Minutes",

                    Minutes =
                        30,

                    IsDefault =
                        false
                },

                new MarketInterval
                {
                    Name =
                        "1 Hour",

                    Minutes =
                        60,

                    IsDefault =
                        false
                },

                new MarketInterval
                {
                    Name =

                    Minutes =

                    IsDefault =

                },

                },

        private static List<MarketPeriod>

            return
            [
                new MarketPeriod
                {
                    Name =
                        "30 Days",

                    Days =
                        30,

                    IsDefault =
                        false
                },

                new MarketPeriod
                {
                    Name =
                        "90 Days",

                    Days =
                        90,

                    IsDefault =
                        false
                },

                new MarketPeriod
                {

                    Days =
                        180,

                    IsDefault =
                        false
                },

                {

                    IsDefault =

                },

                }

        // ==========================================
        // JSON HELPERS
        // ==========================================

        private async Task<JsonDocument> GetJsonAsync(
            string requestUri,
            CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            try
            {
                using HttpResponseMessage response =
                    await _httpClient.GetAsync(
                        requestUri,
                        HttpCompletionOption
                            .ResponseHeadersRead,
                        cancellationToken
                    );


                if (
                    response.StatusCode ==
                    HttpStatusCode.TooManyRequests
                )
                {
                    throw new InvalidOperationException(
                        "Kraken temporarily limited the request rate."
                    );
                }


                response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


                await using System.IO.Stream stream =
                    await response.Content
                        .ReadAsStreamAsync(
                            cancellationToken
                        );


                return await JsonDocument.ParseAsync(
                    stream,
                    cancellationToken:
                        cancellationToken
                );
            }
            catch (TaskCanceledException)
                when (
                    !cancellationToken
                        .IsCancellationRequested
                )
            {
                throw new TimeoutException(
                    "The Kraken request timed out."
                );
            }
            catch (HttpRequestException error)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Kraken could not be reached. " +
                    "Check the internet connection.",
                    error
                );
            }
        }


        private static void ThrowIfKrakenReturnedErrors(
            JsonElement root)
        {
            if (
                !root.TryGetProperty(
                    "error",
                    out JsonElement errorsElement
                )
                ||
                errorsElement.ValueKind !=
                JsonValueKind.Array
            )
            {
                return;
            }


            List<string> errors =
                errorsElement
                    .EnumerateArray()
                    .Where(
                        item =>
                            item.ValueKind ==
                            JsonValueKind.String
                    )
                    .Select(
                        item =>
                            item.GetString()
                            ??
                            ""
                    )
                    .Where(
                        value =>
                            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                                value
                            )
                    )
                    .ToList();


            if (errors.Count > 0)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Kraken API error: " +
                    string.Join(
                        ", ",
                        errors
                    )
                );
            }
        }


        private static string GetString(
            JsonElement element,
            string propertyName)
        {
            if (
                element.TryGetProperty(
                    propertyName,
                    out JsonElement property
                )
                &&
                property.ValueKind ==
                JsonValueKind.String
            )
            {
                return
                    property.GetString()
                    ??
                    "";
            }


            return "";
        }


        private static double ReadDouble(
            JsonElement element)
        {
            if (
                element.ValueKind ==
                JsonValueKind.Number
                &&
                element.TryGetDouble(
                    out double number
                )
            )
            {
                return number;
            }


            if (
                element.ValueKind ==
                JsonValueKind.String
                &&
                double.TryParse(
                    element.GetString(),
                    NumberStyles.Float,
                    CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    out number
                )
            )
            {
                return number;
            }


            throw new FormatException(
                "Kraken returned an invalid numeric value."
            );
        }


        private static long ReadInt64(
            JsonElement element)
        {
            if (
                element.ValueKind ==
                JsonValueKind.Number
                &&
                element.TryGetInt64(
                    out long number
                )
            )
            {
                return number;
            }


            if (
                element.ValueKind ==
                JsonValueKind.String
                &&
                long.TryParse(
                    element.GetString(),
                    NumberStyles.Integer,
                    CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                    out number
                )
            )
            {
                return number;
            }


            throw new FormatException(
                "Kraken returned an invalid integer value."
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // NORMALIZATION
        // ==========================================

        private static bool TrySplitDisplayPair(
            string displayPair,
            out string baseAsset,
            out string quoteAsset)
        {
            baseAsset =
                "";

            quoteAsset =
                "";


            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    displayPair
                )
            )
            {
                return false;
            }


            string[] parts =
                displayPair.Split(
                    '/',
                    2,
                    StringSplitOptions
                        .RemoveEmptyEntries
                    |
                    StringSplitOptions
                        .TrimEntries
                );


            if (parts.Length != 2)
            {
                return false;
            }


            baseAsset =
                parts[0];

            quoteAsset =
                parts[1];


            return true;
        }


        private static string NormalizeAsset(
            string asset)
        {
            string normalized =
                asset
                    .Trim()
                    .ToUpperInvariant();


            if (
                AssetAliases.TryGetValue(
                    normalized,
                    out string? alias
                )
            )
            {
                return alias;
            }


            return normalized;
        }


        private static string ValidatePair(
            string pair)
        {
            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    pair
                )
            )
            {
                throw new ArgumentException(
                    "A market pair is required.",
                    nameof(pair)
                );
            }


            return
                pair
                    .Trim()
                    .ToUpperInvariant();
        }


        private static int ValidateInterval(
            int interval)
        {
            if (
                !ValidIntervals.Contains(
                    interval
                )
            )
            {
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(
                    nameof(interval),
                    interval,
                    "The selected Kraken interval is not supported."
                );
            }


            return interval;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // ERROR RESULTS
        // ==========================================

        private static MarketMetadataResult
            CreateMetadataError(
                string error)
        {
            return new MarketMetadataResult
            {
                Success =
                    false,

                Error =
                    error,

                Markets =
                    [],

                QuoteCurrencies =
                    [],

                Intervals =
                    CreateIntervals(),

                Periods =
                    CreatePeriods()
            };
        }


        private static MarketDataResult
            CreateMarketDataError(
                string pair,
                int interval,
                string error)
        {
            return new MarketDataResult
            {
                Success =
                    false,

                Error =
                    error,

                Pair =
                    pair,

                Interval =
                    interval,

                Candles =
                    []
            };
        }


        private static string GetFriendlyErrorMessage(
            Exception error)
        {
            return error switch
            {
                TimeoutException =>
                    error.Message,

                InvalidOperationException =>
                    error.Message,

                JsonException =>
                    "Kraken returned invalid JSON.",

                FormatException =>
                    error.Message,

                _ =>
                    error.Message
            };
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CLEANUP
        // ==========================================

        public void Dispose()
        {
            _metadataLock.Dispose();

            _marketDataLock.Dispose();


            if (_ownsHttpClient)
            {
                _httpClient.Dispose();
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CACHE KEY
        // ==========================================

        private readonly record struct MarketCacheKey(
            string Pair,
            int Interval
        );
    }
}
