============================================================
FILE: EthStatistics\Views\DashboardView.xaml.cs
============================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Shapes;
using EthStatistics.Models;
using EthStatistics.Services;

namespace EthStatistics.Views
{
    public partial class DashboardView : UserControl
    {
        // ==========================================
        // CONSTANTS
        // ==========================================

        private const int YAxisTickCount =
            5;

        private const int RsiPeriod =
            14;

        private const double MinimumMonthLabelWidth =
            68.0;

        private const double ChartVerticalPadding =
            8.0;


        // ==========================================
        // SERVICES
        // ==========================================

        private readonly StatisticsService
            _statisticsService = new();


        // ==========================================
        // STATE
        // ==========================================

        private IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle>
            _displayedCandles =
                Array.Empty<MarketCandle>();


        // ==========================================
        // CONSTRUCTOR
        // ==========================================

        public DashboardView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            Loaded +=
                DashboardView_Loaded;

            Unloaded +=
                DashboardView_Unloaded;

            MarketDataStore.Instance.Changed +=
                MarketDataStore_Changed;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // LOAD
        // ==========================================

        private void DashboardView_Loaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            RefreshDashboard();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // STORE CHANGED
        // ==========================================

        private void MarketDataStore_Changed(
            object? sender,
            EventArgs e)
        {
            Dispatcher.Invoke(
                RefreshDashboard
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // REFRESH
        // ==========================================

        private void RefreshDashboard()
        {
            MarketDataStore store =
                MarketDataStore.Instance;

            if (store.IsLoading)
            {
                ClearDashboard();

                MarketPairText.Text =
                    $"Loading {GetMarketDisplayName(store)}...";

                MarketStatusText.Text =
                    "Loading";

                MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                    FindBrush(
                        "WarningBrush"
                    );

                return;
            }

            if (
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    store.Error
                )
            )
            {
                ClearDashboard();

                MarketPairText.Text =
                    "Market data unavailable";

                MarketStatusText.Text =
                    "Unavailable";

                MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                    FindBrush(
                        "NegativeBrush"
                    );

                return;
            }

            if (
                store.Candles.Count < 2 ||
                store.Interval is null
            )
            {
                ClearDashboard();

                MarketPairText.Text =
                    "Waiting for market data";

                MarketStatusText.Text =
                    "Waiting";

                MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                    FindBrush(
                        "TextMutedBrush"
                    );

                return;
            }

            try
            {
                _displayedCandles =
                    store.Candles
                        .Where(
                            candle =>
                                double.IsFinite(
                                    candle.Close
                                )
                                &&
                                candle.Close > 0.0
                        )
                        .OrderBy(
                            candle =>
                                candle.Date
                        )
                        .ToList();

                if (_displayedCandles.Count < 2)
                {
                    ClearDashboard();

                    return;
                }

                UpdateMetrics(
                    store
                );

                UpdateMarketStatus(
                    store
                );

                DrawDashboardChart(
                    _displayedCandles
                );
            }
            catch
            {
                ClearDashboard();

                MarketStatusText.Text =
                    "Error";

                MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                    FindBrush(
                        "NegativeBrush"
                    );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // METRICS
        // ==========================================

        private void UpdateMetrics(
            MarketDataStore store)
        {
            StatisticsResult statistics =
                _statisticsService.Calculate(
                    store.Candles,
                    store.Interval!.Value,
                    store.ImportedReturns
                );

            MarketCandle latest =
                store.Candles[^1];

            MarketCandle previous =
                store.Candles[^2];

            double arithmeticReturn =
                previous.Close != 0.0
                    ?
                    (
                        latest.Close -
                        previous.Close
                    )
                    /
                    previous.Close
                    :
                    0.0;

            bool importedReturns =
                store.ImportedValueType ==
                ImportedValueType.PercentageReturn;

            bool isExcel =
                IsExcelDataset(
                    store
                );

            PriceLabel.Text =
                importedReturns
                    ? "CUMULATIVE INDEX"
                    : "LATEST PRICE";

            PriceDescription.Text =
                importedReturns
                    ? "Base index = 100"
                    : isExcel
                        ? "Latest observation"
                        : "Latest daily close";

            PriceValue.Text =
                FormatPrice(
                    latest.Close
                );

            ChangeLabel.Text =
                isExcel
                    ? "LATEST CHANGE"
                    : "DAILY CHANGE";

            ChangeDescription.Text =
                "Previous close to latest close";

            ChangeValue.Text =
                arithmeticReturn.ToString(
                    "+0.00%;-0.00%;0.00%",
                    CultureInfo.CurrentCulture
                );

            ChangeValue.Foreground =
                arithmeticReturn > 0.0
                    ? FindBrush(
                        "PositiveBrush"
                    )
                    : arithmeticReturn < 0.0
                        ? FindBrush(
                            "NegativeBrush"
                        )
                        : FindBrush(
                            "TextPrimaryBrush"
                        );

            ObservationValue.Text =
                store.Candles.Count.ToString(
                    "N0",
                    CultureInfo.CurrentCulture
                );

            VolatilityValue.Text =
                statistics
                    .AnnualizedVolatility
                    .ToString(
                        "P2",
                        CultureInfo.CurrentCulture
                    );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // STATUS
        // ==========================================

        private void UpdateMarketStatus(
            MarketDataStore store)
        {
            bool isExcel =
                IsExcelDataset(
                    store
                );

            string marketName =
                GetMarketDisplayName(
                    store
                );

            string intervalName =
                isExcel
                    ? GetIntervalLabel(
                        store.Interval!.Value
                    )
                    : "Daily";

            MarketPairText.Text =
                $"{marketName} · " +
                $"{intervalName} · " +
                $"{store.Candles.Count:N0} observations";

            MarketStatusText.Text =
                isExcel
                    ? "Imported"
                    : "Updated";

            MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                FindBrush(
                    "PositiveBrush"
                );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // DRAW CHART
        // ==========================================

        private void DrawDashboardChart(
            IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle> candles)
        {
            double width =
                DashboardChartCanvas.ActualWidth;

            double height =
                DashboardChartCanvas.ActualHeight;

            if (
                width <= 1.0 ||
                height <= 1.0 ||
                candles.Count < 2
            )
            {
                return;
            }

            double dataMinimum =
                candles.Min(
                    candle =>
                        candle.Close
                );

            double dataMaximum =
                candles.Max(
                    candle =>
                        candle.Close
                );

            PriceAxisScale axisScale =
                CreateNicePriceScale(
                    dataMinimum,
                    dataMaximum,
                    YAxisTickCount
                );

            DrawYAxis(
                axisScale
            );

            DrawPriceLine(
                candles,
                axisScale,
                width,
                height
            );

            DrawMeanLine(
                candles,
                axisScale,
                width,
                height
            );

            DrawRsiChart(
                candles
            );

            DrawMonthlyAxis(
                candles,
                width,
                height
            );

            DashboardPriceLine.Visibility =
                Visibility.Visible;

            DashboardEmptyState.Visibility =
                Visibility.Collapsed;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // PRICE LINE
        // ==========================================

        private void DrawPriceLine(
            IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle> candles,
            PriceAxisScale axisScale,
            double width,
            double height)
        {
            double drawableHeight =
                Math.Max(
                    1.0,
                    height -
                    ChartVerticalPadding * 2.0
                );

            double axisRange =
                axisScale.Maximum -
                axisScale.Minimum;

            if (axisRange <= 0.0)
            {
                axisRange =
                    1.0;
            }

            var points =
                new PointCollection(
                    candles.Count
                );

            for (
                int index = 0;
                index < candles.Count;
                index++
            )
            {
                double x =
                    index *
                    width /
                    (candles.Count - 1.0);

                double normalizedPrice =
                    (
                        candles[index].Close -
                        axisScale.Minimum
                    )
                    /
                    axisRange;

                double y =
                    ChartVerticalPadding +
                    (
                        1.0 -
                        normalizedPrice
                    )
                    *
                    drawableHeight;

                points.Add(
                    new Point(
                        x,
                        y
                    )
                );
            }

            DashboardPriceLine.Points =
                points;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // MEAN LINE
        // ==========================================

        private void DrawMeanLine(
            IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle> candles,
            PriceAxisScale axisScale,
            double width,
            double height)
        {
            // Calculated by C++ Mean().

            double meanPrice =
                _statisticsService.CalculateMeanPrice(
                    candles
                );

            if (!double.IsFinite(meanPrice))
            {
                ClearMeanLine();

                return;
            }

            double range =
                axisScale.Maximum -
                axisScale.Minimum;

            if (range <= 0.0)
            {
                ClearMeanLine();

                return;
            }

            double drawableHeight =
                Math.Max(
                    1.0,
                    height -
                    ChartVerticalPadding * 2.0
                );

            double normalizedMean =
                (
                    meanPrice -
                    axisScale.Minimum
                )
                /
                range;

            double y =
                ChartVerticalPadding +
                (
                    1.0 -
                    normalizedMean
                )
                *
                drawableHeight;

            DashboardMeanLine.X1 =
                0.0;

            DashboardMeanLine.X2 =
                width;

            DashboardMeanLine.Y1 =
                y;

            DashboardMeanLine.Y2 =
                y;

            DashboardMeanLine.Visibility =
                Visibility.Visible;

            MeanLegendText.Text =
                $"x̄ = {FormatPrice(meanPrice)}";
        }


        // ==========================================
        // RSI
        // ==========================================

        private void DrawRsiChart(
     IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle> candles)
        {
            double width =
                RsiSeriesCanvas.ActualWidth;

            double height =
                RsiSeriesCanvas.ActualHeight;

            RsiSeriesCanvas.Children.Clear();

            if (
                width <= 1.0 ||
                height <= 1.0
            )
            {
                Dispatcher.BeginInvoke(
                    new Action(
                        () =>
                        {
                            if (_displayedCandles.Any())
                            {
                                DrawDashboardChart(
                                    _displayedCandles
                                );
                            }
                        }
                    ),
                    System.Windows.Threading.DispatcherPriority.Loaded
                );

                return;
            }

            if (candles.Count <= RsiPeriod)
            {
                ClearRsiChart();

                return;
            }

            double[] rsiValues =
                _statisticsService.CalculateWilderRsi(
                    candles,
                    RsiPeriod
                );

            if (
                rsiValues.Length !=
                candles.Count
            )
            {
                ClearRsiChart();

                return;
            }

            DrawRsiReferenceLine(
                Rsi70Line,
                70.0,
                width,
                height
            );

            DrawRsiReferenceLine(
                Rsi50Line,
                50.0,
                width,
                height
            );

            DrawRsiReferenceLine(
                Rsi30Line,
                30.0,
                width,
                height
            );

            for (
                int index = 1;
                index < rsiValues.Length;
                index++
            )
            {
                double previousRsi =
                    rsiValues[index - 1];

                double currentRsi =
                    rsiValues[index];

                if (
                    !double.IsFinite(previousRsi) ||
                    !double.IsFinite(currentRsi)
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }

                previousRsi =
                    Math.Clamp(
                        previousRsi,
                        0.0,
                        100.0
                    );

                currentRsi =
                    Math.Clamp(
                        currentRsi,
                        0.0,
                        100.0
                    );

                double x1 =
                    (index - 1.0)
                    *
                    width
                    /
                    (rsiValues.Length - 1.0);

                double x2 =
                    index
                    *
                    width
                    /
                    (rsiValues.Length - 1.0);

                double y1 =
                    ConvertRsiToY(
                        previousRsi,
                        height
                    );

                double y2 =
                    ConvertRsiToY(
                        currentRsi,
                        height
                    );

                DrawRsiSegment(
                    x1,
                    y1,
                    x2,
                    y2,
                    previousRsi,
                    currentRsi
                );
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // RSI COORDINATE
        // ==========================================

        private static double ConvertRsiToY(
            double rsi,
            double height)
        {
            return
                (
                    1.0 -
                    rsi / 100.0
                )
                *
                height;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // RSI SEGMENT
        // ==========================================

        private void DrawRsiSegment(
            double x1,
            double y1,
            double x2,
            double y2,
            double previousRsi,
            double currentRsi)
        {
            // One daily move could theoretically
            // cross both thresholds.
            //
            // Process 30 and 70 as break points.

            List<double> thresholds =
                new();

            if (
                CrossesThreshold(
                    previousRsi,
                    currentRsi,
                    30.0
                )
            )
            {
                thresholds.Add(
                    30.0
                );
            }

            if (
                CrossesThreshold(
                    previousRsi,
                    currentRsi,
                    70.0
                )
            )
            {
                thresholds.Add(
                    70.0
                );
            }

            if (thresholds.Count == 0)
            {
                AddRsiLine(
                    x1,
                    y1,
                    x2,
                    y2,
                    GetRsiBrush(
                        (
                            previousRsi +
                            currentRsi
                        )
                        /
                        2.0
                    )
                );

                return;
            }

            if (currentRsi < previousRsi)
            {
                thresholds =
                    thresholds
                        .OrderByDescending(
                            value =>
                                value
                        )
                        .ToList();
            }
            else
            {
                thresholds =
                    thresholds
                        .OrderBy(
                            value =>
                                value
                        )
                        .ToList();
            }

            double startX =
                x1;

            double startY =
                y1;

            double startRsi =
                previousRsi;

            foreach (
                double threshold
                in thresholds
            )
            {
                double totalRsiDifference =
                    currentRsi -
                    previousRsi;

                if (
                    Math.Abs(
                        totalRsiDifference
                    )
                    <
                    double.Epsilon
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }

                double fraction =
                    (
                        threshold -
                        previousRsi
                    )
                    /
                    totalRsiDifference;

                double crossingX =
                    x1
                    +
                    fraction
                    *
                    (
                        x2 -
                        x1
                    );

                double crossingY =
                    y1
                    +
                    fraction
                    *
                    (
                        y2 -
                        y1
                    );

                double middleRsi =
                    (
                        startRsi +
                        threshold
                    )
                    /
                    2.0;

                AddRsiLine(
                    startX,
                    startY,
                    crossingX,
                    crossingY,
                    GetRsiBrush(
                        middleRsi
                    )
                );

                startX =
                    crossingX;

                startY =
                    crossingY;

                startRsi =
                    threshold;
            }

            double finalMiddleRsi =
                (
                    startRsi +
                    currentRsi
                )
                /
                2.0;

            AddRsiLine(
                startX,
                startY,
                x2,
                y2,
                GetRsiBrush(
                    finalMiddleRsi
                )
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // RSI THRESHOLD
        // ==========================================

        private static bool CrossesThreshold(
            double first,
            double second,
            double threshold)
        {
            return
                (
                    first < threshold &&
                    second >= threshold
                )
                ||
                (
                    first > threshold &&
                    second <= threshold
                );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // RSI COLOR
        // ==========================================

        private Brush GetRsiBrush(
            double rsi)
        {
            // Overbought.

            if (rsi > 70.0)
            {
                return FindBrush(
                    "NegativeBrush"
                );
            }

            // Oversold.

            if (rsi < 30.0)
            {
                return FindBrush(
                    "PositiveBrush"
                );
            }

            // Neutral.

            return FindBrush(
                "AccentBrush"
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // RSI LINE
        // ==========================================

        private void AddRsiLine(
       double x1,
       double y1,
       double x2,
       double y2,
       Brush brush)
        {
            var line =
                new Line
                {
                    X1 =
                        x1,

                    Y1 =
                        y1,

                    X2 =
                        x2,

                    Y2 =
                        y2,

                    Stroke =
                        brush,

                    StrokeThickness =
                        2.0,

                    StrokeStartLineCap =
                        PenLineCap.Round,

                    StrokeEndLineCap =
                        PenLineCap.Round,

                    IsHitTestVisible =
                        false
                };

            RsiSeriesCanvas.Children.Add(
                line
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // RSI REFERENCE LINES
        // ==========================================

        private static void DrawRsiReferenceLine(
            Line line,
            double value,
            double width,
            double height)
        {
            double y =
                ConvertRsiToY(
                    value,
                    height
                );

            line.X1 =
                0.0;

            line.X2 =
                width;

            line.Y1 =
                y;

            line.Y2 =
                y;

            line.Visibility =
                Visibility.Visible;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // Y AXIS
        // ==========================================

        private void DrawYAxis(
            PriceAxisScale scale)
        {
            double[] values =
            {
                scale.Maximum,
                scale.Maximum - scale.Step,
                scale.Maximum - scale.Step * 2.0,
                scale.Maximum - scale.Step * 3.0,
                scale.Minimum
            };

            YAxisMaximumText.Text =
                FormatAxisPrice(
                    values[0],
                    scale.Step
                );

            YAxisUpperMiddleText.Text =
                FormatAxisPrice(
                    values[1],
                    scale.Step
                );

            YAxisMiddleText.Text =
                FormatAxisPrice(
                    values[2],
                    scale.Step
                );

            YAxisLowerMiddleText.Text =
                FormatAxisPrice(
                    values[3],
                    scale.Step
                );

            YAxisMinimumText.Text =
                FormatAxisPrice(
                    values[4],
                    scale.Step
                );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // NICE PRICE SCALE
        // ==========================================

        private static PriceAxisScale
            CreateNicePriceScale(
                double dataMinimum,
                double dataMaximum,
                int tickCount)
        {
            if (
                !double.IsFinite(
                    dataMinimum
                )
                ||
                !double.IsFinite(
                    dataMaximum
                )
            )
            {
                return new PriceAxisScale(
                    0.0,
                    1.0,
                    0.25
                );
            }

            if (dataMaximum < dataMinimum)
            {
                (
                    dataMinimum,
                    dataMaximum
                ) =
                (
                    dataMaximum,
                    dataMinimum
                );
            }

            double rawRange =
                dataMaximum -
                dataMinimum;

            if (rawRange <= 0.0)
            {
                double expansion =
                    Math.Abs(
                        dataMaximum
                    )
                    *
                    0.05;

                if (expansion <= 0.0)
                {
                    expansion =
                        1.0;
                }

                dataMinimum -=
                    expansion;

                dataMaximum +=
                    expansion;

                rawRange =
                    dataMaximum -
                    dataMinimum;
            }

            int intervalCount =
                Math.Max(
                    1,
                    tickCount - 1
                );

            double rawStep =
                rawRange /
                intervalCount;

            double niceStep =
                CalculateNiceStep(
                    rawStep
                );

            double niceMinimum =
                Math.Floor(
                    dataMinimum /
                    niceStep
                )
                *
                niceStep;

            double niceMaximum =
                Math.Ceiling(
                    dataMaximum /
                    niceStep
                )
                *
                niceStep;

            int actualIntervals =
                (int)Math.Ceiling(
                    (
                        niceMaximum -
                        niceMinimum
                    )
                    /
                    niceStep
                );

            if (actualIntervals < intervalCount)
            {
                niceMaximum =
                    niceMinimum
                    +
                    niceStep
                    *
                    intervalCount;
            }
            else if (actualIntervals > intervalCount)
            {
                niceStep =
                    CalculateNiceStep(
                        (
                            niceMaximum -
                            niceMinimum
                        )
                        /
                        intervalCount
                    );

                niceMinimum =
                    Math.Floor(
                        dataMinimum /
                        niceStep
                    )
                    *
                    niceStep;

                niceMaximum =
                    niceMinimum
                    +
                    niceStep
                    *
                    intervalCount;

                while (
                    niceMaximum <
                    dataMaximum
                )
                {
                    niceMinimum -=
                        niceStep;

                    niceMaximum =
                        niceMinimum
                        +
                        niceStep
                        *
                        intervalCount;
                }
            }

            return new PriceAxisScale(
                niceMinimum,
                niceMaximum,
                niceStep
            );
        }


        private static double CalculateNiceStep(
            double rawStep)
        {
            if (
                !double.IsFinite(
                    rawStep
                )
                ||
                rawStep <= 0.0
            )
            {
                return 1.0;
            }

            double exponent =
                Math.Floor(
                    Math.Log10(
                        rawStep
                    )
                );

            double magnitude =
                Math.Pow(
                    10.0,
                    exponent
                );

            double normalizedStep =
                rawStep /
                magnitude;

            double niceNormalizedStep;

            if (normalizedStep <= 1.0)
            {
                niceNormalizedStep =
                    1.0;
            }
            else if (normalizedStep <= 2.0)
            {
                niceNormalizedStep =
                    2.0;
            }
            else if (normalizedStep <= 2.5)
            {
                niceNormalizedStep =
                    2.5;
            }
            else if (normalizedStep <= 5.0)
            {
                niceNormalizedStep =
                    5.0;
            }
            else
            {
                niceNormalizedStep =
                    10.0;
            }

            return
                niceNormalizedStep *
                magnitude;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // MONTHLY X AXIS
        // ==========================================

        private void DrawMonthlyAxis(
            IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle> candles,
            double width,
            double graphHeight)
        {
            MonthlyGridCanvas.Children.Clear();

            MonthlyLabelsCanvas.Children.Clear();

            if (
                candles.Count < 2 ||
                width <= 1.0
            )
            {
                return;
            }

            List<MonthTick> allMonthTicks =
                CreateMonthTicks(
                    candles
                );

            if (allMonthTicks.Count == 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            int maximumVisibleLabelCount =
                Math.Max(
                    2,
                    (int)Math.Floor(
                        width /
                        MinimumMonthLabelWidth
                    )
                );

            int labelStep =
                Math.Max(
                    1,
                    (int)Math.Ceiling(
                        allMonthTicks.Count /
                        (double)maximumVisibleLabelCount
                    )
                );

            List<MonthTick> visibleTicks =
                allMonthTicks
                    .Where(
                        (
                            tick,
                            index
                        ) =>
                            index % labelStep ==
                            0
                    )
                    .ToList();

            MonthTick lastMonthTick =
                allMonthTicks[^1];

            if (
                visibleTicks.Count == 0 ||
                visibleTicks[^1].Year !=
                    lastMonthTick.Year ||
                visibleTicks[^1].Month !=
                    lastMonthTick.Month
            )
            {
                visibleTicks.Add(
                    lastMonthTick
                );
            }

            bool multipleYears =
                candles[0].Date.Year !=
                candles[^1].Date.Year;

            foreach (
                MonthTick tick
                in visibleTicks
            )
            {
                double x =
                    tick.CandleIndex
                    *
                    width
                    /
                    (candles.Count - 1.0);

                DrawMonthlyGridLine(
                    x,
                    graphHeight
                );

                DrawMonthLabel(
                    tick,
                    x,
                    width,
                    multipleYears
                );
            }
        }


        private static List<MonthTick>
            CreateMonthTicks(
                IReadOnlyList<MarketCandle> candles)
        {
            var ticks =
                new List<MonthTick>();

            int? previousYear =
                null;

            int? previousMonth =
                null;

            for (
                int index = 0;
                index < candles.Count;
                index++
            )
            {
                DateTimeOffset date =
                    candles[index].Date;

                if (
                    previousYear ==
                        date.Year
                    &&
                    previousMonth ==
                        date.Month
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }

                ticks.Add(
                    new MonthTick(
                        index,
                        date.Year,
                        date.Month
                    )
                );

                previousYear =
                    date.Year;

                previousMonth =
                    date.Month;
            }

            return ticks;
        }


        private void DrawMonthlyGridLine(
            double x,
            double graphHeight)
        {
            var line =
                new Line
                {
                    X1 =
                        x,

                    X2 =
                        x,

                    Y1 =
                        0.0,

                    Y2 =
                        graphHeight,

                    Stroke =
                        FindBrush(
                            "BorderSubtleBrush"
                        ),

                    StrokeThickness =
                        1.0,

                    IsHitTestVisible =
                        false
                };

            MonthlyGridCanvas.Children.Add(
                line
            );
        }


        private void DrawMonthLabel(
            MonthTick tick,
            double x,
            double totalWidth,
            bool includeYear)
        {
            DateTime labelDate =
                new(
                    tick.Year,
                    tick.Month,
                    1
                );

            string label =
                includeYear
                    ? labelDate.ToString(
                        "MMM yy",
                        CultureInfo.CurrentCulture
                    )
                    : labelDate.ToString(
                        "MMM",
                        CultureInfo.CurrentCulture
                    );

            var textBlock =
                new TextBlock
                {
                    Text =
                        label,

                    Foreground =
                        FindBrush(
                            "TextMutedBrush"
                        ),

                    FontSize =
                        9.0,

                    IsHitTestVisible =
                        false
                };

            textBlock.Measure(
                new Size(
                    double.PositiveInfinity,
                    double.PositiveInfinity
                )
            );

            double labelWidth =
                textBlock.DesiredSize.Width;

            double left =
                x -
                labelWidth / 2.0;

            left =
                Math.Max(
                    0.0,
                    Math.Min(
                        totalWidth -
                        labelWidth,
                        left
                    )
                );

            Canvas.SetLeft(
                textBlock,
                left
            );

            Canvas.SetTop(
                textBlock,
                7.0
            );

            MonthlyLabelsCanvas.Children.Add(
                textBlock
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // RESIZE
        // ==========================================

        private void DashboardChart_SizeChanged(
            object sender,
            SizeChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!_displayedCandles.Any())
            {
                return;
            }

            DrawDashboardChart(
                _displayedCandles
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // DISPLAY HELPERS
        // ==========================================

        private static bool IsExcelDataset(
            MarketDataStore store)
        {
            return (
                store.DataSource
                ??
                ""
            ).StartsWith(
                "Excel",
                StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
            );
        }


        private static string GetMarketDisplayName(
            MarketDataStore store)
        {
            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    store.Pair
                )
            )
            {
                return "Market";
            }

            KrakenMarket? market =
                store.Markets.FirstOrDefault(
                    item =>
                        string.Equals(
                            item.Pair,
                            store.Pair,
                            StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                        )
                );

            if (
                market is not null &&
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    market.DisplayName
                )
            )
            {
                return market.DisplayName;
            }

            return store.Pair;
        }


        private static string GetIntervalLabel(
            int interval)
        {
            return interval switch
            {
                1 =>
                    "1 Minute",

                5 =>
                    "5 Minutes",

                15 =>
                    "15 Minutes",

                30 =>
                    "30 Minutes",

                60 =>
                    "1 Hour",

                240 =>
                    "4 Hours",

                1440 =>
                    "Daily",

                10080 =>
                    "Weekly",

                21600 =>
                    "15 Days",

                _ =>
                    $"{interval} min"
            };
        }


        private static string FormatPrice(
            double value)
        {
            double absoluteValue =
                Math.Abs(
                    value
                );

            if (absoluteValue >= 1.0)
            {
                return value.ToString(
                    "N2",
                    CultureInfo.CurrentCulture
                );
            }

            return value.ToString(
                "N6",
                CultureInfo.CurrentCulture
            );
        }


        private static string FormatAxisPrice(
            double value,
            double step)
        {
            double absoluteStep =
                Math.Abs(
                    step
                );

            string format =
                absoluteStep >= 1.0
                    ? "N0"
                    : absoluteStep >= 0.01
                        ? "N2"
                        : "N6";

            return value.ToString(
                format,
                CultureInfo.CurrentCulture
            );
        }


        private Brush FindBrush(
            string resourceName)
        {
            return (Brush)FindResource(
                resourceName
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CLEAR MEAN
        // ==========================================

        private void ClearMeanLine()
        {
            DashboardMeanLine.X1 =
                0.0;

            DashboardMeanLine.X2 =
                0.0;

            DashboardMeanLine.Y1 =
                0.0;

            DashboardMeanLine.Y2 =
                0.0;

            DashboardMeanLine.Visibility =
                Visibility.Collapsed;

            MeanLegendText.Text =
                "";
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CLEAR RSI
        // ==========================================

        private void ClearRsiChart()
        {
            RsiSeriesCanvas.Children.Clear();

            ClearLine(
                Rsi70Line
            );

            ClearLine(
                Rsi50Line
            );

            ClearLine(
                Rsi30Line
            );
        }


        private static void ClearLine(
            Line line)
        {
            line.X1 =
                0.0;

            line.X2 =
                0.0;

            line.Y1 =
                0.0;

            line.Y2 =
                0.0;

            line.Visibility =
                Visibility.Collapsed;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CLEAR DASHBOARD
        // ==========================================

        private void ClearDashboard()
        {
            _displayedCandles =
                Array.Empty<MarketCandle>();

            PriceValue.Text =
                "—";

            ChangeValue.Text =
                "—";

            ObservationValue.Text =
                "—";

            VolatilityValue.Text =
                "—";

            ChangeValue.Foreground =
                FindBrush(
                    "TextPrimaryBrush"
                );

            DashboardPriceLine.Points =
                new PointCollection();

            DashboardPriceLine.Visibility =
                Visibility.Collapsed;

            ClearMeanLine();

            ClearRsiChart();

            DashboardEmptyState.Visibility =
                Visibility.Visible;

            YAxisMaximumText.Text =
                "";

            YAxisUpperMiddleText.Text =
                "";

            YAxisMiddleText.Text =
                "";

            YAxisLowerMiddleText.Text =
                "";

            YAxisMinimumText.Text =
                "";

            MonthlyGridCanvas.Children.Clear();

            MonthlyLabelsCanvas.Children.Clear();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // CLEANUP
        // ==========================================

        private void DashboardView_Unloaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            MarketDataStore.Instance.Changed -=
                MarketDataStore_Changed;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // TYPES
        // ==========================================

        private sealed record PriceAxisScale(
            double Minimum,
            double Maximum,
            double Step
        );


        private sealed record MonthTick(
            int CandleIndex,
            int Year,
            int Month
        );
    }
}

                <TextBlock Text="1. Statistical Foundations"
                           Style="{StaticResource DictionarySectionHeadingStyle}"
                           Margin="0,10,0,12"/>


                <Expander x:Name="PopulationTopic"
                          Header="Population, sample and observation"
                          IsExpanded="True"
                          Style="{StaticResource DictionaryExpanderStyle}">

                    <Border Style="{StaticResource CardStyle}">

                        <StackPanel>

                            <TextBlock Text="Population and sample"
                                       Style="{StaticResource DictionaryTopicHeadingStyle}"
                                       Margin="0,0,0,6"/>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="A population is the complete collection of units or outcomes about which an investigation seeks to draw conclusions. A sample is the subset that is observed and used to learn about that population."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <TextBlock Text="Sample: X₁, X₂, …, Xₙ"
                                           Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                            </Border>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                Text="The integer n denotes the sample size. Before observation, each Xᵢ is treated as a random variable. After data are collected, its realised value is written xᵢ."/>

                        </StackPanel>

                    </Border>

                </Expander>


                <Expander x:Name="MeanTopic"
                          Header="Arithmetic mean"
                          Style="{StaticResource DictionaryExpanderStyle}">

                    <Border Style="{StaticResource CardStyle}">

                        <StackPanel>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="The arithmetic mean summarises the central level of a sample. It incorporates every observation, making it informative but sensitive to unusually large or small values."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <TextBlock Text="x̄ = (1/n) Σᵢ₌₁ⁿ xᵢ"
                                           Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                            </Border>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource DictionaryInsightPanelStyle}">

                                <TextBlock
                                    Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                    Text="The sample mean x̄ is a statistic and is commonly used as an estimator of the population mean μ."/>

                            </Border>

                        </StackPanel>

                    </Border>

                </Expander>


                <Expander x:Name="VarianceTopic"
                          Header="Variance and standard deviation"
                          Style="{StaticResource DictionaryExpanderStyle}">

                    <Border Style="{StaticResource CardStyle}">

                        <StackPanel>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Variance measures dispersion through squared deviations from the sample mean. Standard deviation is the square root of variance and therefore expresses dispersion in the original measurement units."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="s² = [1/(n - 1)] Σᵢ₌₁ⁿ (xᵢ - x̄)²"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="s = √s²"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                        Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource DictionaryInsightPanelStyle}">

                                <TextBlock
                                    Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                    Text="The denominator n - 1 is the degrees-of-freedom correction used by the unbiased sample-variance estimator under standard independent-sampling assumptions."/>

                            </Border>

                        </StackPanel>

                    </Border>

                </Expander>


                <Expander x:Name="ReturnsTopic"
                          Header="Financial returns"
                          Style="{StaticResource DictionaryExpanderStyle}">

                    <Border Style="{StaticResource CardStyle}">

                        <StackPanel>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="A simple return measures proportional price change between two periods. A logarithmic return measures the change in log price and is additive across consecutive periods, making it useful in financial time-series modelling."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="Simple return: Rₜ = (Pₜ - Pₜ₋₁)/Pₜ₋₁"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="Log return: rₜ = ln(Pₜ/Pₜ₋₁)"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                        Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                Text="Log returns are additive across time: the multi-period log return equals the sum of the constituent single-period log returns."/>

                        </StackPanel>

                    </Border>

                </Expander>


                <Expander x:Name="ShapeTopic"
                          Header="Skewness, kurtosis and quantiles"
                          Style="{StaticResource DictionaryExpanderStyle}">

                    <Border Style="{StaticResource CardStyle}">

                        <StackPanel>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Skewness describes distributional asymmetry. Kurtosis describes the relative concentration of probability in a distribution's centre and tails. A p-quantile is a threshold below which probability p lies."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <TextBlock Text="Quantile qₚ: P(X ≤ qₚ) = p"
                                           Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                            </Border>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource DictionaryInsightPanelStyle}">

                                <TextBlock
                                    Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                    Text="Quantiles are useful for asymmetric or heavy-tailed distributions because they provide a direct probability-based description of location."/>

                            </Border>

                        </StackPanel>

                    </Border>

                </Expander>
