                <!-- STATISTICAL INFERENCE -->
                <!-- ========================================== -->

                <TextBlock Text="3. Statistical Inference"
                           Style="{StaticResource DictionarySectionHeadingStyle}"/>


                <Expander x:Name="HypothesisBasicsTopic"
                          Header="Hypotheses, significance and decision errors"
                          IsExpanded="True"
                          Style="{StaticResource DictionaryExpanderStyle}">

                    <Border Style="{StaticResource CardStyle}">

                        <StackPanel>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="The null hypothesis H₀ defines the reference model against which the observed data are evaluated. The alternative hypothesis H₁ describes the departure of substantive interest. The significance level α is the maximum long-run probability of rejecting H₀ when H₀ is true."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <TextBlock Text="Reject H₀ when p-value ≤ α"
                                           Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                            </Border>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource DictionarySubtlePanelStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Type I error"
                                               Foreground="{DynamicResource TextPrimaryBrush}"
                                               FontWeight="SemiBold"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                        Text="Rejecting H₀ when H₀ is true."
                                        Margin="0,4,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Type II error"
                                               Foreground="{DynamicResource TextPrimaryBrush}"
                                               FontWeight="SemiBold"
                                               Margin="0,12,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                        Text="Failing to reject H₀ when the specified alternative is true."
                                        Margin="0,4,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource DictionaryInsightPanelStyle}">

                                <TextBlock
                                    Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                    Text="Failing to reject H₀ does not demonstrate that H₀ is true. It means that the available sample did not provide sufficient evidence for rejection at the selected significance level."/>

                            </Border>

                        </StackPanel>

                    </Border>

                </Expander>


                <!-- ========================================== -->
                <!-- CONFIDENCE INTERVALS -->
                <!-- ========================================== -->

                <Expander x:Name="ConfidenceIntervalTopic"
                          Header="Confidence intervals from first principles"
                          IsExpanded="True"
                          Style="{StaticResource DictionaryExpanderStyle}">

                    <Border Style="{StaticResource CardStyle}">

                        <StackPanel>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Suppose X₁, X₂, …, Xₙ are independent observations from a normal population N(μ, σ²). The objective is to construct an interval estimator for the unknown population mean μ."/>

                            <TextBlock Text="Step 1 — Begin with the sample mean"
                                       Style="{StaticResource DictionaryTopicHeadingStyle}"/>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="The sample mean X̄ is the natural estimator of μ. However, it is not itself a pivot because its sampling distribution still depends on the unknown parameter μ."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="X̄ ~ N(μ, σ²/n)"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="E[X̄] = μ"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Var(X̄) = σ²/n"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>


                            <TextBlock
                                Text="Case 1 — Known population standard deviation"
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryTopicHeadingStyle}"/>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Standardising X̄ by subtracting μ and dividing by its known standard error produces a pivot. The expression contains μ, but its probability distribution is completely known and does not depend on unknown parameters."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Z = (X̄ - μ)/(σ/√n)"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Z ~ N(0,1)"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Select constants c₁ and c₂ from the standard normal distribution so that the probability enclosed between them equals the desired confidence coefficient 1 - α."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="P(c₁ ≤ Z ≤ c₂) = 1 - α"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="P(c₁ ≤ (X̄ - μ)/(σ/√n) ≤ c₂) = 1 - α"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                        Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Algebraically rearranging the inequalities isolates μ and transforms the probability statement about the pivot into an interval estimator."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <TextBlock
                                    Text="P(X̄ - c₂σ/√n ≤ μ ≤ X̄ - c₁σ/√n) = 1 - α"
                                    Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                            </Border>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="For a symmetric two-sided interval, c₁ = -zα/2 and c₂ = zα/2."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <TextBlock
                                    Text="μ ∈ [X̄ - zα/2 σ/√n, X̄ + zα/2 σ/√n]"
                                    Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                            </Border>


                            <TextBlock
                                Text="Case 2 — Unknown population standard deviation"
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryTopicHeadingStyle}"/>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="When σ is unknown, it is replaced by the sample standard deviation S. The additional uncertainty introduced by estimating σ changes the pivot's distribution from standard normal to Student's t with n - 1 degrees of freedom."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="S² = [1/(n - 1)] Σᵢ₌₁ⁿ (Xᵢ - X̄)²"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="T = (X̄ - μ)/(S/√n)"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                        Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="T ~ tₙ₋₁"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                        Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="The degrees of freedom equal n - 1 because the deviations used to calculate S² must sum to zero after X̄ has been estimated."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="P(-tα/2,n-1 ≤ T ≤ tα/2,n-1) = 1 - α"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="μ ∈ [X̄ - tα/2,n-1 S/√n, X̄ + tα/2,n-1 S/√n]"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                        Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource DictionaryInsightPanelStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Known σ"
                                               Foreground="{DynamicResource TextPrimaryBrush}"
                                               FontWeight="SemiBold"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                        Text="Use the standard normal pivot and standard normal critical values."
                                        Margin="0,4,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Unknown σ"
                                               Foreground="{DynamicResource TextPrimaryBrush}"
                                               FontWeight="SemiBold"
                                               Margin="0,10,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                        Text="Use the Student t pivot and t critical values with n - 1 degrees of freedom."
                                        Margin="0,4,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>


                            <TextBlock
                                Text="Numerical example — 95% interval with unknown σ"
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryTopicHeadingStyle}"
                                Margin="0,20,0,6"/>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Suppose a normal sample gives n = 25, x̄ = 52 and s = 10. Since σ is unknown, use the t distribution with 24 degrees of freedom."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="n = 25,   x̄ = 52,   s = 10"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="df = n - 1 = 24"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="t₀.₉₇₅,₂₄ ≈ 2.064"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="SE = s/√n = 10/√25 = 2"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Margin = 2.064 × 2 = 4.128"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource DictionarySubtlePanelStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="95% confidence interval"
                                               Foreground="{DynamicResource TextPrimaryBrush}"
                                               FontWeight="SemiBold"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="52 ± 4.128"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,9,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="[47.872, 56.128]"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,9,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                Margin="0,10,0,0"
                                Text="The population parameter μ is fixed. The interval endpoints vary across repeated samples. Under the model assumptions, 95% of intervals produced by this procedure contain μ."/>

                        </StackPanel>

                    </Border>

                </Expander>


                <!-- ========================================== -->
                <!-- P-VALUE -->
                <!-- ========================================== -->

                <Expander x:Name="PValueTopic"
                          Header="How is a p-value obtained?"
                          IsExpanded="True"
                          Style="{StaticResource DictionaryExpanderStyle}">

                    <Border Style="{StaticResource CardStyle}">

                        <StackPanel>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="A p-value quantifies the extremeness of the observed test statistic relative to its distribution under the null hypothesis. It is the probability, calculated under H₀, of obtaining a statistic at least as incompatible with H₀ as the one observed."/>

                            <TextBlock Text="Step 1 — Specify the null distribution"
                                       Style="{StaticResource DictionaryTopicHeadingStyle}"/>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Suppose H₀ states that μ = μ₀. When σ is unknown and the observations are normally distributed, the test statistic follows Student's t distribution under H₀."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="T = (X̄ - μ₀)/(S/√n)"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="T ~ tₙ₋₁ under H₀"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>


                            <TextBlock Text="Step 2 — Calculate the observed statistic"
                                       Style="{StaticResource DictionaryTopicHeadingStyle}"/>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Substituting the observed sample quantities produces Tobs, the realised value of the test statistic. Its position in the null distribution determines the relevant tail probability."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <TextBlock
                                    Text="Tobs = (x̄obs - μ₀)/(s/√n)"
                                    Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                            </Border>


                            <TextBlock Text="Step 3 — Calculate the tail probability"
                                       Style="{StaticResource DictionaryTopicHeadingStyle}"/>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="The definition of an outcome at least as extreme as the observation depends on the direction of the alternative hypothesis."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="Right-tailed: p = P(T ≥ Tobs | H₀)"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="Left-tailed: p = P(T ≤ Tobs | H₀)"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                        Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Text="Two-sided: p = 2P(T ≥ |Tobs| | H₀)"
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                        Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>


                            <TextBlock
                                Text="Numerical example — right-tailed one-sample t-test"
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryTopicHeadingStyle}"
                                Margin="0,20,0,6"/>

                            <TextBlock
                                Style="{StaticResource DictionaryBodyTextStyle}"
                                Text="Use the sample n = 25, x̄ = 52 and s = 10. Test H₀: μ = 50 against H₁: μ &gt; 50."/>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource FormulaBoxStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="H₀: μ = 50"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="H₁: μ &gt; 50"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Tobs = (52 - 50)/(10/√25)"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Tobs = 1"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="df = 24"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="p = P(T₂₄ ≥ 1) ≈ 0.1636"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,8,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource DictionarySubtlePanelStyle}">

                                <StackPanel>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Decision at α = 0.05"
                                               Foreground="{DynamicResource TextPrimaryBrush}"
                                               FontWeight="SemiBold"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="0.1636 &gt; 0.05"
                                               Style="{StaticResource DictionaryFormulaTextStyle}"
                                               Margin="0,9,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock Text="Fail to reject H₀."
                                               Foreground="{DynamicResource TextSecondaryBrush}"
                                               FontWeight="SemiBold"
                                               Margin="0,9,0,0"/>

                                    <TextBlock
                                        Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                        Text="The sample does not provide sufficiently strong evidence at the 5% level that μ is greater than 50."
                                        Margin="0,6,0,0"/>

                                </StackPanel>

                            </Border>

                            <Border Style="{StaticResource DictionaryInsightPanelStyle}">

                                <TextBlock
                                    Style="{StaticResource DictionarySupportingTextStyle}"
                                    Text="A small p-value indicates that the observed result would be unusual under H₀. It provides evidence against H₀, but it is not the probability that H₀ is true and does not by itself measure practical importance."/>

                            </Border>

                        </StackPanel>

                    </Border>

                </Expander>


                <!-- ========================================== -->

FILE: EthStatistics\Views\DictionaryView.xaml.cs
