FILE: EthStatistics\Views\SettingsView.xaml.cs
============================================================
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using EthStatistics.Services;
using EthStatistics.Settings;

namespace EthStatistics.Views
{
    public partial class SettingsView : UserControl
    {
        // ==========================================
        // SERVICES / STATE
        // ==========================================

        private readonly UserPreferencesService
            _preferencesService;

        private readonly UserPreferences
            _preferences;

        private readonly string?
            _accountId;

        private bool
            _initialized;


        // ==========================================
        // EVENTS
        // ==========================================

        public event EventHandler<DataPreferencesChangedEventArgs>?
            DataPreferencesChanged;

        public event EventHandler<GeneralPreferencesChangedEventArgs>?
            GeneralPreferencesChanged;


        // ==========================================
        // CONSTRUCTOR
        // ==========================================

        public SettingsView(
            UserPreferences preferences,
            UserPreferencesService preferencesService,
            string? accountId)
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            _preferences =
                preferences;

            _preferencesService =
                preferencesService;

            _accountId =
                accountId;


            Loaded +=
                SettingsView_Loaded;

            Unloaded +=
                SettingsView_Unloaded;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // LOAD
        // ==========================================

        private void SettingsView_Loaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            InitializeControls();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // INITIALIZATION
        // ==========================================

        private void InitializeControls()
        {
            _initialized =
                false;


            RemoveControlEvents();


            PopulateLanguageOptions();

            PopulateThemeOptions();

            PopulateMarketOptions();


            AddControlEvents();


            _initialized =
                true;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // LANGUAGE
        // ==========================================

        private void PopulateLanguageOptions()
        {
            LanguageComboBox.ItemsSource =
                new[]
                {
                    "English",
                    "Greek"
                };


            if (
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    _preferences.Language
                )
                &&
                LanguageComboBox.Items.Contains(
                    _preferences.Language
                )
            )
            {
                LanguageComboBox.SelectedItem =
                    _preferences.Language;
            }
            else if (
                LanguageComboBox.Items.Count > 0
            )
            {
                LanguageComboBox.SelectedIndex =
                    0;
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // THEME
        // ==========================================

        private void PopulateThemeOptions()
        {
            ThemeComboBox.ItemsSource =
                new[]
                {
                    "Dark",
                    "Light",
                    "System"
                };


            if (
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    _preferences.Theme
                )
                &&
                ThemeComboBox.Items.Contains(
                    _preferences.Theme
                )
            )
            {
                ThemeComboBox.SelectedItem =
                    _preferences.Theme;
            }
            else if (
                ThemeComboBox.Items.Count > 0
            )
            {
                ThemeComboBox.SelectedIndex =
                    0;
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // MARKET OPTIONS
        // ==========================================

        private void PopulateMarketOptions()
        {
            MarketDataStore store =
                MarketDataStore.Instance;


            CurrencyComboBox.ItemsSource =
                store.QuoteCurrencies;


            if (
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    _preferences.QuoteCurrency
                )
                &&
                store.QuoteCurrencies.Contains(
                    _preferences.QuoteCurrency
                )
            )
            {
                CurrencyComboBox.SelectedItem =
                    _preferences.QuoteCurrency;
            }
            else if (
                CurrencyComboBox.Items.Count > 0
            )
            {
                CurrencyComboBox.SelectedIndex =
                    0;
            }
        }


        // ==========================================
        // EVENTS
        // ==========================================

        private void AddControlEvents()
        {
            LanguageComboBox.SelectionChanged +=
                GeneralPreference_SelectionChanged;


            ThemeComboBox.SelectionChanged +=
                GeneralPreference_SelectionChanged;


            CurrencyComboBox.SelectionChanged +=
                DataPreference_SelectionChanged;
        }


        private void RemoveControlEvents()
        {
            LanguageComboBox.SelectionChanged -=
                GeneralPreference_SelectionChanged;


            ThemeComboBox.SelectionChanged -=
                GeneralPreference_SelectionChanged;


            CurrencyComboBox.SelectionChanged -=
                DataPreference_SelectionChanged;
        }


        // ==========================================
        // GENERAL PREFERENCES
        // ==========================================

        private async void GeneralPreference_SelectionChanged(
            object sender,
            SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!_initialized)
            {
                return;
            }


            UpdatePreferencesFromControls();


            await SavePreferencesAsync();


            GeneralPreferencesChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new GeneralPreferencesChangedEventArgs
                {
                    Language =
                        _preferences.Language,

                    Theme =
                        _preferences.Theme
                }
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // DATA PREFERENCES
        // ==========================================

        private async void DataPreference_SelectionChanged(
            object sender,
            SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            if (!_initialized)
            {
                return;
            }


            UpdatePreferencesFromControls();


            await SavePreferencesAsync();


            RaiseDataPreferencesChanged();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // UPDATE PREFERENCES
        // ==========================================

        private void UpdatePreferencesFromControls()
        {
            _preferences.Language =
                LanguageComboBox
                    .SelectedItem?
                    .ToString();


            _preferences.Theme =
                ThemeComboBox
                    .SelectedItem?
                    .ToString();


            _preferences.QuoteCurrency =
                CurrencyComboBox
                    .SelectedItem?
                    .ToString();
        }


        // ==========================================
        // SAVE PROFILE
        // ==========================================

        private async Task SavePreferencesAsync()
        {
            await _preferencesService.SaveAsync(
                _preferences,
                _accountId
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // DATA EVENT
        // ==========================================

        private void RaiseDataPreferencesChanged()
        {
            if (
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    _preferences.QuoteCurrency
                )
            )
            {
                return;
            }


            DataPreferencesChanged?.Invoke(
                this,
                new DataPreferencesChangedEventArgs
                {
                    QuoteCurrency =
                        _preferences.QuoteCurrency
                }
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // SAVE BUTTON
        // ==========================================

        private async void SavePreferences_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            UpdatePreferencesFromControls();


            await SavePreferencesAsync();


            string profileDescription =
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    _accountId
                )
                    ? "Guest preferences saved."
                    : "Your preferences have been saved.";


            MessageBox.Show(
                profileDescription,
                "Settings",
                MessageBoxButton.OK,
                MessageBoxImage.Information
            );
        }


        // ==========================================
        // UNLOAD
        // ==========================================

        private void SettingsView_Unloaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            RemoveControlEvents();
        }
    }
}

FILE: EthStatistics\Views\SignedOutView.xaml.cs
============================================================
using System;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;

namespace EthStatistics.Views
{
    public partial class SignedOutView : UserControl
    {
        public event EventHandler?
            SignInRequested;


        public SignedOutView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();
        }


        private void SignInButton_Click(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            SignInRequested?.Invoke(
                this,
                EventArgs.Empty
            );
        }


        public void SetLoading(
            bool isLoading)
        {
            SignInButton.IsEnabled =
                !isLoading;

            LoadingPanel.Visibility =
                isLoading
                    ? Visibility.Visible
                    : Visibility.Collapsed;
        }
    }
}

FILE: EthStatistics\Views\StatisticsView.xaml.cs
============================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Windows;
using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Shapes;
using EthStatistics.Models;
using EthStatistics.Services;

namespace EthStatistics.Views
{
    public partial class StatisticsView : UserControl
    {
        private readonly StatisticsService _statisticsService = new();

        public StatisticsView()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            Loaded += StatisticsView_Loaded;
            Unloaded += StatisticsView_Unloaded;

            MarketDataStore.Instance.Changed +=
                MarketDataStore_Changed;
        }

        private void StatisticsView_Loaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            RefreshStatistics();
        }

        private void MarketDataStore_Changed(
            object? sender,
            EventArgs e)
        {
            Dispatcher.Invoke(
                RefreshStatistics
            );
        }

        private void RefreshStatistics()
        {
            MarketDataStore store =
                MarketDataStore.Instance;

            if (store.IsLoading)
            {
                MarketAnalysisText.Text =
                    $"Loading {store.Pair}...";

                MarketStatusText.Text =
                    "LOADING";

                MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                    FindBrush("WarningBrush");

                ClearStatistics();

                return;
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(store.Error))
            {
                MarketAnalysisText.Text =
                    "Market data unavailable";

                MarketStatusText.Text =
                    "UNAVAILABLE";

                MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                    FindBrush("NegativeBrush");

                ClearStatistics();

                return;
            }

            if (
                store.Candles.Count < 2 ||
                store.Interval is null
            )
            {
                MarketAnalysisText.Text =
                    "Waiting for market data";

                MarketStatusText.Text =
                    "WAITING";

                MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                    FindBrush("TextMutedBrush");

                ClearStatistics();

                return;
            }

            try
            {
                StatisticsResult result =
                    _statisticsService.Calculate(
                        store.Candles,
                        store.Interval.Value,
                        store.ImportedReturns
                    );

                double[] returns =
                    GetReturns(store);

                MarketAnalysisText.Text =
                    $"{store.Pair} · {store.DataSource}";

                MarketStatusText.Text =
                    store.DataSource.StartsWith(
                        "Excel",
                        StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase
                    )
                        ? "IMPORTED"
                        : "UPDATED";

                MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                    FindBrush("PositiveBrush");

                DistributionMarketText.Text =
                    store.Pair ?? "—";

                MeanValue.Text =
                    result.MeanPrice.ToString("N2");

                MinimumValue.Text =
                    result.MinimumPrice.ToString("N2");

                MaximumValue.Text =
                    result.MaximumPrice.ToString("N2");

                ObservationCountValue.Text =
                    result.ObservationCount.ToString();

                MeanReturnValue.Text =
                    result.MeanLogReturn.ToString("P4");

                VarianceValue.Text =
                    result.ReturnVariance.ToString("G6");

                StandardDeviationValue.Text =
                    result.ReturnStandardDeviation.ToString("P2");

                VolatilityValue.Text =
                    result.AnnualizedVolatility.ToString("P2");

                Quantile05Value.Text =
                    result.Quantile05.ToString("P2");

                Quantile10Value.Text =
                    result.Quantile10.ToString("P2");

                Quantile90Value.Text =
                    result.Quantile90.ToString("P2");

                Quantile95Value.Text =
                    result.Quantile95.ToString("P2");

                SkewnessValue.Text =
                    $"Skewness: {result.Skewness:N3}";

                KurtosisValue.Text =
                    result.ExcessKurtosis.ToString("N3");

                KurtosisDescriptionText.Text =
                    result.ExcessKurtosis switch
                    {
                        > 0.5 =>
                            "Heavier than normal tails",

                        < -0.5 =>
                            "Lighter than normal tails",

                        _ =>
                            "Near normal tail weight"
                    };

                DistributionShapeText.Text =
                    result.DistributionShape;

                DrawReturnDistribution(
                    returns
                );
            }
            catch (Exception error)
            {
                MarketAnalysisText.Text =
                    "Statistics unavailable";

                MarketStatusText.Text =
                    "ERROR";

                MarketStatusIndicator.Fill =
                    FindBrush("NegativeBrush");

                ClearStatistics();

                ToolTipService.SetToolTip(
                    MarketStatusText,
                    error.Message
                );
            }
        }

        private static double[] GetReturns(
            MarketDataStore store)
        {
            if (store.ImportedReturns.Count > 0)
            {
                return store.ImportedReturns
                    .Where(double.IsFinite)
                    .ToArray();
            }

            if (store.Candles.Count < 2)
            {
                return [];
            }

            var returns =
                new List<double>(
                    store.Candles.Count - 1
                );

            for (
                int index = 1;
                index < store.Candles.Count;
                index++
            )
            {
                double previousPrice =
                    store.Candles[index - 1].Close;

                double currentPrice =
                    store.Candles[index].Close;

                if (
                    previousPrice <= 0 ||
                    currentPrice <= 0
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }

                double logReturn =
                    Math.Log(
                        currentPrice /
                        previousPrice
                    );

                if (double.IsFinite(logReturn))
                {
                    returns.Add(logReturn);
                }
            }

            return returns.ToArray();
        }

        private void DrawReturnDistribution(
            IReadOnlyList<double> returns)
        {
            DistributionCanvas.Children.Clear();

            double width =
                DistributionCanvas.ActualWidth;

            double height =
                DistributionCanvas.ActualHeight;

            if (
                width <= 1 ||
                height <= 1
            )
            {
                return;
            }

            double[] validReturns =
                returns
                    .Where(double.IsFinite)
                    .ToArray();

            if (validReturns.Length < 2)
            {
                ShowEmptyDistribution();

                return;
            }

            double minimum =
                validReturns.Min();

            double maximum =
                validReturns.Max();

            double range =
                maximum - minimum;

            if (
                !double.IsFinite(range) ||
                range <= 0
            )
            {
                ShowEmptyDistribution();

                return;
            }

            int binCount =
                Math.Clamp(
                    (int)Math.Ceiling(
                        Math.Sqrt(
                            validReturns.Length
                        )
                    ),
                    5,
                    40
                );

            double binWidth =
                range /
                binCount;

            if (
                !double.IsFinite(binWidth) ||
                binWidth <= 0
            )
            {
                ShowEmptyDistribution();

                return;
            }

            int[] counts =
                new int[binCount];

            foreach (double value in validReturns)
            {
                int binIndex =
                    (int)(
                        (value - minimum) /
                        binWidth
                    );

                if (binIndex >= binCount)
                {
                    binIndex =
                        binCount - 1;
                }

                if (binIndex < 0)
                {
                    binIndex = 0;
                }

                counts[binIndex]++;
            }

            int maximumCount =
                counts.Max();

            if (maximumCount <= 0)
            {
                ShowEmptyDistribution();

                return;
            }

            double visualBarWidth =
                width /
                binCount;

            double maximumBarHeight =
                height * 0.92;

            for (
                int index = 0;
                index < binCount;
                index++
            )
            {
                int count =
                    counts[index];

                double heightRatio =
                    (double)count /
                    maximumCount;

                double barHeight =
                    heightRatio *
                    maximumBarHeight;

                double lowerBound =
                    minimum +
                    index * binWidth;

                double upperBound =
                    index == binCount - 1
                        ? maximum
                        : lowerBound + binWidth;

                double observationPercentage =
                    (double)count /
                    validReturns.Length;

                var tooltipContent =
                    new StackPanel();

                tooltipContent.Children.Add(
                    new TextBlock
                    {
                        Text =
                            $"{lowerBound:P3} to {upperBound:P3}",

                        FontWeight =
                            FontWeights.SemiBold
                    }
                );

                tooltipContent.Children.Add(
                    new TextBlock
                    {
                        Text =
                            $"Observations: {count}",

                        Margin =
                            new Thickness(0, 5, 0, 0)
                    }
                );

                tooltipContent.Children.Add(
                    new TextBlock
                    {
                        Text =
                            $"Share: {observationPercentage:P2}",

                        Margin =
                            new Thickness(0, 2, 0, 0)
                    }
                );

                var rectangle =
                    new Rectangle
                    {
                        Width =
                            Math.Max(
                                1,
                                visualBarWidth - 2
                            ),

                        Height =
                            Math.Max(
                                0,
                                barHeight
                            ),

                        Fill =
                            FindBrush("AccentBrush"),

                        Opacity =
                            0.72,

                        RadiusX = 2,
                        RadiusY = 2,

                        Cursor =
                            Cursors.Hand,

                        ToolTip =
                            new ToolTip
                            {
                                Content =
                                    tooltipContent
                            }
                    };

                rectangle.MouseEnter +=
                    (_, _) =>
                    {
                        rectangle.Opacity = 1.0;
                    };

                rectangle.MouseLeave +=
                    (_, _) =>
                    {
                        rectangle.Opacity = 0.72;
                    };

                Canvas.SetLeft(
                    rectangle,
                    index *
                    visualBarWidth
                );

                Canvas.SetTop(
                    rectangle,
                    height -
                    barHeight
                );

                DistributionCanvas.Children.Add(
                    rectangle
                );
            }

            MinimumReturnText.Text =
                minimum.ToString("P2");

            MaximumReturnText.Text =
                maximum.ToString("P2");

            EmptyDistributionState.Visibility =
                Visibility.Collapsed;
        }

        private void ShowEmptyDistribution()
        {
            DistributionCanvas.Children.Clear();

            MinimumReturnText.Text = "";
            MaximumReturnText.Text = "";

            EmptyDistributionState.Visibility =
                Visibility.Visible;
        }

        private void DistributionChart_SizeChanged(
            object sender,
            SizeChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            MarketDataStore store =
                MarketDataStore.Instance;

            if (
                store.IsLoading ||
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(
                    store.Error
                )
            )
            {
                return;
            }

            double[] returns =
                GetReturns(store);

            if (returns.Length > 1)
            {
                DrawReturnDistribution(
                    returns
                );
            }
            else
            {
                ShowEmptyDistribution();
            }
        }

        private void ClearStatistics()
        {
            MeanValue.Text = "—";
            MinimumValue.Text = "—";
            MaximumValue.Text = "—";
            ObservationCountValue.Text = "—";

            MeanReturnValue.Text = "—";
            VarianceValue.Text = "—";
            StandardDeviationValue.Text = "—";
            VolatilityValue.Text = "—";

            Quantile05Value.Text = "—";
            Quantile10Value.Text = "—";
            Quantile90Value.Text = "—";
            Quantile95Value.Text = "—";

            SkewnessValue.Text = "—";
            KurtosisValue.Text = "—";

            KurtosisDescriptionText.Text =
                "Tail weight";

            DistributionShapeText.Text =
                "—";

            DistributionMarketText.Text =
                "—";

            ShowEmptyDistribution();
        }

        private Brush FindBrush(
            string resourceName)
        {
            return (Brush)FindResource(
                resourceName
            );
        }

        private void StatisticsView_Unloaded(
            object sender,
            RoutedEventArgs e)
        {
            MarketDataStore.Instance.Changed -=
                MarketDataStore_Changed;
        }
    }
}

<Package xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/appx/manifest/foundation/windows10" xmlns:uap="http://schemas.microsoft.com/appx/manifest/uap/windows10" xmlns:rescap="http://schemas.microsoft.com/appx/manifest/foundation/windows10/restrictedcapabilities" IgnorableNamespaces="uap rescap">

import "./App.css";

function App() {
    const downloadApp = async () => {
        try {
            const metadataUrl =
                `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}downloads/latest-version.json?nocache=${Date.now()}`;

            const response = await fetch(metadataUrl, {
                cache: "no-store",
            });

            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Could not load the latest release: ${response.status}`
                );
            }

            const release = await response.json();

            if (
                !release.downloadUrl ||
                typeof release.downloadUrl !== "string"
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "The release metadata does not contain a download URL."
                );
            }

            const downloadUrl = new URL(release.downloadUrl);

            if (
                downloadUrl.protocol !== "https:" ||
                downloadUrl.hostname !== "microsoftfodev.github.io" ||
                !downloadUrl.pathname
                    .toLowerCase()
                    .endsWith(".msixbundle")
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "The release download URL is not valid."
                );
            }

            window.location.href = downloadUrl.href;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(
                "EthStatistics download failed:",
                error
            );

            alert(
                "The latest EthStatistics package could not be downloaded. Please try again shortly."
            );
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="site">
            {/* NAVBAR */}
            <header className="navbar">
                <div className="navInner">
                    <a className="logo" href="#home">
                        <div className="logoMark">
                            <span>Îž</span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="logoText">
                            <strong>EthStatistics</strong>
                            <small>Quantitative Ethereum Analytics</small>
                        </div>
                    </a>

                    <nav className="navLinks">
                        <a href="#features">Features</a>
                        <a href="#technology">Technology</a>
                        <a href="#download">Download</a>
                    </nav>

                    <button className="navDownload" onClick={downloadApp}>
                        Download
                    </button>
                </div>
            </header>

            <main>
                {/* HERO */}
                <section className="hero" id="home">
                    <div className="heroGrid" />
                    <div className="heroGlow heroGlowPurple" />

                    <div className="heroContent">
                        <div className="eyebrow">
                            <span className="statusDot" />
                            ETHEREUM QUANTITATIVE ANALYTICS
                        </div>

                        <h1>
                            Understand Ethereum
                            <span> through statistics.</span>
                        </h1>

                        <p className="heroText">
                            A desktop analytics platform combining Ethereum market data,
                            statistical analysis, technical indicators and quantitative
                            tools in one clean environment.
                        </p>

                        <div className="heroActions">
                            <button className="buttonPrimary" onClick={downloadApp}>
                                <span className="buttonIcon">â†“</span>
                                Download for Windows
                            </button>

                            <a className="buttonSecondary" href="#features">
                                Explore platform
                                <span>â†’</span>

// dllmain.cpp : Defines the entry point for the DLL application.
#include "pch.h"

BOOL APIENTRY DllMain( HMODULE hModule,
                       DWORD  ul_reason_for_call,
                       LPVOID lpReserved
                     )
{
    switch (ul_reason_for_call)
    {
    case DLL_PROCESS_ATTACH:
    case DLL_THREAD_ATTACH:
    case DLL_THREAD_DETACH:
    case DLL_PROCESS_DETACH:
        break;
    }
    return TRUE;
}

============================================================
FILE: QuantEngine\QuantEngine.cpp

#include "pch.h"
#include "QuantEngine.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#ifdef min
#undef min
#endif

#ifdef max
#undef max
#endif
namespace
{
    // ==========================================
    // INTERNAL HELPERS
    // ==========================================

    double MissingValue()
    {
        return std::numeric_limits<double>
            ::quiet_NaN();
    }


    bool IsValidArray(
        const double* values,
        int size)
    {
        if (
            values == nullptr ||
            size <= 0
            )
        {
            return false;
        }


        for (
            int index = 0;
            index < size;
            index++
            )
        {
            if (!std::isfinite(values[index]))
            {
                return false;
            }
        }


        return true;
    }


    bool AreValidPrices(
        const double* prices,
        int size)
    {
        if (!IsValidArray(prices, size))
        {
            return false;
        }


        for (
            int index = 0;
            index < size;
            index++
            )
        {
            if (prices[index] <= 0.0)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }


        return true;
    }


    void InitializeOutput(
        double* output,
        int size)
    {
        if (
            output == nullptr ||
            size <= 0
            )
        {
            return;
        }


        for (
            int index = 0;
            index < size;
            index++
            )
        {
            output[index] =
                MissingValue();
        }
    }


    double CalculateRsiValue(
        double averageGain,
        double averageLoss)
    {
        if (
            averageGain <= 0.0 &&
            averageLoss <= 0.0
            )
        {
            return 50.0;
        }


        if (averageLoss <= 0.0)
        {
            return 100.0;
        }


        if (averageGain <= 0.0)
        {
            return 0.0;
        }


        const double relativeStrength =
            averageGain /
            averageLoss;


        return
            100.0 -
            (
                100.0 /
                (
                    1.0 +
                    relativeStrength
                    )
                );
    }
}


// ==========================================
// ADDITION
// ==========================================

double Add(
    double a,
    double b)
{
    return a + b;
}


// ==========================================
// ARITHMETIC MEAN
// ==========================================
//
// x_bar = (1 / n) * sum(x_i)
//
// ==========================================

double Mean(
    const double* values,
    int size)
{
    if (!IsValidArray(values, size))
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    double sum =
        0.0;


    for (
        int index = 0;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        sum +=
            values[index];
    }


    return
        sum /
        static_cast<double>(size);
}


// ==========================================
// GEOMETRIC MEAN
// ==========================================
//
// G = (product(x_i))^(1 / n)
//
// Numerically stable form:
//
// G = exp(
//     (1 / n) * sum(log(x_i))
// )
//
// ==========================================

double GeometricMean(
    const double* values,
    int size)
{
    if (!IsValidArray(values, size))
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    double sumOfLogs =
        0.0;


    for (
        int index = 0;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double value =
            values[index];


        if (value <= 0.0)
        {
            return MissingValue();
        }


        sumOfLogs +=
            std::log(value);
    }


    const double meanLog =
        sumOfLogs /
        static_cast<double>(size);


    const double result =
        std::exp(meanLog);


    return
        std::isfinite(result)
        ? result
        : MissingValue();
}


// ==========================================
// HARMONIC MEAN
// ==========================================
//
// H = n / sum(1 / x_i)
//
// ==========================================

double HarmonicMean(
    const double* values,
    int size)
{
    if (!IsValidArray(values, size))
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    double reciprocalSum =
        0.0;


    for (
        int index = 0;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double value =
            values[index];


        if (value <= 0.0)
        {
            return MissingValue();
        }


        reciprocalSum +=
            1.0 /
            value;
    }


    if (
        !std::isfinite(reciprocalSum) ||
        reciprocalSum <= 0.0
        )
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    const double result =
        static_cast<double>(size) /
        reciprocalSum;


    return
        std::isfinite(result)
        ? result
        : MissingValue();
}


// ==========================================
// WEIGHTED MEAN
// ==========================================
//
// x_w =
// sum(w_i * x_i) / sum(w_i)
//
// ==========================================

double WeightedMean(
    const double* values,
    const double* weights,
    int size)
{
    if (
        !IsValidArray(values, size) ||
        !IsValidArray(weights, size)
        )
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    double weightedSum =
        0.0;


    double totalWeight =
        0.0;


    for (
        int index = 0;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double weight =
            weights[index];


        if (weight < 0.0)
        {
            return MissingValue();
        }


        weightedSum +=
            values[index] *
            weight;


        totalWeight +=
            weight;
    }


    if (
        !std::isfinite(totalWeight) ||
        totalWeight <= 0.0
        )
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    const double result =
        weightedSum /
        totalWeight;


    return
        std::isfinite(result)
        ? result
        : MissingValue();
}


// ==========================================
// SAMPLE VARIANCE
// ==========================================
//
// s^2 =
// sum((x_i - x_bar)^2) / (n - 1)
//
// ==========================================

double Variance(
    const double* values,
    int size)
{
    if (
        !IsValidArray(values, size) ||
        size < 2
        )
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    const double mean =
        Mean(
            values,
            size
        );


    if (!std::isfinite(mean))
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    double sumSquaredDifferences =
        0.0;


    for (
        int index = 0;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double difference =
            values[index] -
            mean;


        sumSquaredDifferences +=
            difference *
            difference;
    }


    const double result =
        sumSquaredDifferences /
        static_cast<double>(
            size - 1
            );


    return
        std::isfinite(result)
        ? result
        : MissingValue();
}


// ==========================================
// SAMPLE STANDARD DEVIATION
// ==========================================

double StandardDeviation(
    const double* values,
    int size)
{
    const double variance =
        Variance(
            values,
            size
        );


    if (
        !std::isfinite(variance) ||
        variance < 0.0
        )
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    return std::sqrt(
        variance
    );
}


// ==========================================
// LOG RETURN
// ==========================================
//
// r_t = log(P_t / P_(t-1))
//
// ==========================================

double LogReturn(
    double previousPrice,
    double currentPrice)
{
    if (
        !std::isfinite(previousPrice) ||
        !std::isfinite(currentPrice) ||
        previousPrice <= 0.0 ||
        currentPrice <= 0.0
        )
    {
        return MissingValue();
    }


    return std::log(
        currentPrice /
        previousPrice
    );
}


// ==========================================
// WILDER RSI
// ==========================================

void WilderRSI(
    const double* prices,
    int size,
    int period,
    double* output)
{
    if (
        output == nullptr ||
        size <= 0
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    InitializeOutput(
        output,
        size
    );


    if (
        !AreValidPrices(prices, size) ||
        period <= 0 ||
        size <= period
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    double gainSum =
        0.0;


    double lossSum =
        0.0;


    for (
        int index = 1;
        index <= period;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double change =
            prices[index] -
            prices[index - 1];


        if (change > 0.0)
        {
            gainSum +=
                change;
        }
        else if (change < 0.0)
        {
            lossSum +=
                -change;
        }
    }


    double averageGain =
        gainSum /
        static_cast<double>(period);


    double averageLoss =
        lossSum /
        static_cast<double>(period);


    output[period] =
        CalculateRsiValue(
            averageGain,
            averageLoss
        );


    for (
        int index = period + 1;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double change =
            prices[index] -
            prices[index - 1];


        const double gain =
            std::max(
                change,
                0.0
            );


        const double loss =
            std::max(
                -change,
                0.0
            );


        averageGain =
            (
                averageGain *
                static_cast<double>(
                    period - 1
                    )
                +
                gain
                )
            /
            static_cast<double>(period);


        averageLoss =
            (
                averageLoss *
                static_cast<double>(
                    period - 1
                    )
                +
                loss
                )
            /
            static_cast<double>(period);


        output[index] =
            CalculateRsiValue(
                averageGain,
                averageLoss
            );
    }
}


// ==========================================
// CUTLER RSI
// ==========================================

void CutlerRSI(
    const double* prices,
    int size,
    int period,
    double* output)
{
    if (
        output == nullptr ||
        size <= 0
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    InitializeOutput(
        output,
        size
    );


    if (
        !AreValidPrices(prices, size) ||
        period <= 0 ||
        size <= period
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    double gainSum =
        0.0;


    double lossSum =
        0.0;


    for (
        int index = 1;
        index <= period;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double change =
            prices[index] -
            prices[index - 1];


        gainSum +=
            std::max(
                change,
                0.0
            );


        lossSum +=
            std::max(
                -change,
                0.0
            );
    }


    output[period] =
        CalculateRsiValue(
            gainSum /
            static_cast<double>(period),
            lossSum /
            static_cast<double>(period)
        );


    for (
        int index = period + 1;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double newChange =
            prices[index] -
            prices[index - 1];


        const double oldChange =
            prices[index - period] -
            prices[index - period - 1];


        gainSum +=
            std::max(
                newChange,
                0.0
            );


        lossSum +=
            std::max(
                -newChange,
                0.0
            );


        gainSum -=
            std::max(
                oldChange,
                0.0
            );


        lossSum -=
            std::max(
                -oldChange,
                0.0
            );


        // Avoid insignificant negative values
        // caused by floating-point subtraction.

        gainSum =
            std::max(
                gainSum,
                0.0
            );


        lossSum =
            std::max(
                lossSum,
                0.0
            );


        output[index] =
            CalculateRsiValue(
                gainSum /
                static_cast<double>(period),
                lossSum /
                static_cast<double>(period)
            );
    }
}


// ==========================================
// EXPONENTIAL RSI
// ==========================================

void ExponentialRSI(
    const double* prices,
    int size,
    int period,
    double* output)
{
    if (
        output == nullptr ||
        size <= 0
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    InitializeOutput(
        output,
        size
    );


    if (
        !AreValidPrices(prices, size) ||
        period <= 0 ||
        size <= period
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    double gainSum =
        0.0;


    double lossSum =
        0.0;


    for (
        int index = 1;
        index <= period;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double change =
            prices[index] -
            prices[index - 1];


        gainSum +=
            std::max(
                change,
                0.0
            );


        lossSum +=
            std::max(
                -change,
                0.0
            );
    }


    double averageGain =
        gainSum /
        static_cast<double>(period);


    double averageLoss =
        lossSum /
        static_cast<double>(period);


    output[period] =
        CalculateRsiValue(
            averageGain,
            averageLoss
        );


    const double alpha =
        2.0 /
        (
            static_cast<double>(period) +
            1.0
            );


    for (
        int index = period + 1;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double change =
            prices[index] -
            prices[index - 1];


        const double gain =
            std::max(
                change,
                0.0
            );


        const double loss =
            std::max(
                -change,
                0.0
            );


        averageGain =
            alpha *
            gain
            +
            (
                1.0 -
                alpha
                )
            *
            averageGain;


        averageLoss =
            alpha *
            loss
            +
            (
                1.0 -
                alpha
                )
            *
            averageLoss;


        output[index] =
            CalculateRsiValue(
                averageGain,
                averageLoss
            );
    }
}


// ==========================================
// STOCHASTIC RSI
// ==========================================

void StochasticRSI(
    const double* prices,
    int size,
    int rsiPeriod,
    int stochasticPeriod,
    double* output)
{
    if (
        output == nullptr ||
        size <= 0
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    InitializeOutput(
        output,
        size
    );


    if (
        !AreValidPrices(prices, size) ||
        rsiPeriod <= 0 ||
        stochasticPeriod <= 0 ||
        size <= rsiPeriod
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    std::vector<double> rsi(
        static_cast<std::size_t>(size)
    );


    WilderRSI(
        prices,
        size,
        rsiPeriod,
        rsi.data()
    );


    const int firstIndex =
        rsiPeriod +
        stochasticPeriod -
        1;


    if (firstIndex >= size)
    {
        return;
    }


    for (
        int index = firstIndex;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const int startIndex =
            index -
            stochasticPeriod +
            1;


        double minimumRsi =
            std::numeric_limits<double>
            ::infinity();


        double maximumRsi =
            -std::numeric_limits<double>
            ::infinity();


        bool validWindow =
            true;


        for (
            int windowIndex = startIndex;
            windowIndex <= index;
            windowIndex++
            )
        {
            const double value =
                rsi[
                    static_cast<std::size_t>(
                        windowIndex
                        )
                ];


            if (!std::isfinite(value))
            {
                validWindow =
                    false;

                break;
            }


            minimumRsi =
                std::min(
                    minimumRsi,
                    value
                );


            maximumRsi =
                std::max(
                    maximumRsi,
                    value
                );
        }


        if (!validWindow)
        {
            continue;
        }


        const double range =
            maximumRsi -
            minimumRsi;


        if (range <= 0.0)
        {
            output[index] =
                0.0;
        }
        else
        {
            output[index] =
                100.0 *
                (
                    rsi[
                        static_cast<std::size_t>(
                            index
                            )
                    ]
                    -
                    minimumRsi
                    )
                /
                range;
        }
    }
}


// ==========================================
// PRICE STREAK
// ==========================================

void PriceStreak(
    const double* prices,
    int size,
    double* output)
{
    if (
        output == nullptr ||
        size <= 0
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    InitializeOutput(
        output,
        size
    );


    if (!AreValidPrices(prices, size))
    {
        return;
    }


    output[0] =
        0.0;


    for (
        int index = 1;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        if (
            prices[index] >
            prices[index - 1]
            )
        {
            output[index] =
                output[index - 1] > 0.0
                ? output[index - 1] + 1.0
                : 1.0;
        }
        else if (
            prices[index] <
            prices[index - 1]
            )
        {
            output[index] =
                output[index - 1] < 0.0
                ? output[index - 1] - 1.0
                : -1.0;
        }
        else
        {
            output[index] =
                0.0;
        }
    }
}


// ==========================================
// PERCENT RANK
// ==========================================

void PercentRank(
    const double* values,
    int size,
    int period,
    double* output)
{
    if (
        output == nullptr ||
        size <= 0
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    InitializeOutput(
        output,
        size
    );


    if (
        values == nullptr ||
        period <= 0 ||
        size <= period
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    for (
        int index = period;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double currentValue =
            values[index];


        if (!std::isfinite(currentValue))
        {
            continue;
        }


        const int startIndex =
            index -
            period;


        int lowerCount =
            0;


        int equalCount =
            0;


        bool validWindow =
            true;


        for (
            int windowIndex = startIndex;
            windowIndex < index;
            windowIndex++
            )
        {
            const double historicalValue =
                values[windowIndex];


            if (!std::isfinite(historicalValue))
            {
                validWindow =
                    false;

                break;
            }


            if (
                historicalValue <
                currentValue
                )
            {
                lowerCount++;
            }
            else if (
                historicalValue ==
                currentValue
                )
            {
                equalCount++;
            }
        }


        if (!validWindow)
        {
            continue;
        }


        output[index] =
            100.0 *
            (
                static_cast<double>(
                    lowerCount
                    )
                +
                0.5 *
                static_cast<double>(
                    equalCount
                    )
                )
            /
            static_cast<double>(period);
    }
}


// ==========================================
// CONNORS RSI
// ==========================================

void ConnorsRSI(
    const double* prices,
    int size,
    int priceRsiPeriod,
    int streakRsiPeriod,
    int rankPeriod,
    double* output)
{
    if (
        output == nullptr ||
        size <= 0
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    InitializeOutput(
        output,
        size
    );


    if (
        !AreValidPrices(prices, size) ||
        priceRsiPeriod <= 0 ||
        streakRsiPeriod <= 0 ||
        rankPeriod <= 0
        )
    {
        return;
    }


    const std::size_t vectorSize =
        static_cast<std::size_t>(size);


    std::vector<double> priceRsi(
        vectorSize
    );


    std::vector<double> streak(
        vectorSize
    );


    std::vector<double> streakRsi(
        vectorSize
    );


    std::vector<double> returns(
        vectorSize,
        MissingValue()
    );


    std::vector<double> percentRank(
        vectorSize
    );


    WilderRSI(
        prices,
        size,
        priceRsiPeriod,
        priceRsi.data()
    );


    PriceStreak(
        prices,
        size,
        streak.data()
    );


    // Streaks may be negative, so use the RSI
    // function directly. WilderRSI validates
    // prices as positive, therefore we calculate
    // streak RSI locally here.

    if (size > streakRsiPeriod)
    {
        double gainSum =
            0.0;


        double lossSum =
            0.0;


        std::fill(
            streakRsi.begin(),
            streakRsi.end(),
            MissingValue()
        );


        for (
            int index = 1;
            index <= streakRsiPeriod;
            index++
            )
        {
            const double change =
                streak[
                    static_cast<std::size_t>(
                        index
                        )
                ]
                -
                streak[
                    static_cast<std::size_t>(
                        index - 1
                        )
                ];


            gainSum +=
                std::max(
                    change,
                    0.0
                );


            lossSum +=
                std::max(
                    -change,
                    0.0
                );
        }


        double averageGain =
            gainSum /
            static_cast<double>(
                streakRsiPeriod
                );


        double averageLoss =
            lossSum /
            static_cast<double>(
                streakRsiPeriod
                );


        streakRsi[
            static_cast<std::size_t>(
                streakRsiPeriod
                )
        ] =
            CalculateRsiValue(
                averageGain,
                averageLoss
            );


            for (
                int index = streakRsiPeriod + 1;
                index < size;
                index++
                )
            {
                const double change =
                    streak[
                        static_cast<std::size_t>(
                            index
                            )
                    ]
                    -
                    streak[
                        static_cast<std::size_t>(
                            index - 1
                            )
                    ];


                const double gain =
                    std::max(
                        change,
                        0.0
                    );


                const double loss =
                    std::max(
                        -change,
                        0.0
                    );


                averageGain =
                    (
                        averageGain *
                        static_cast<double>(
                            streakRsiPeriod - 1
                            )
                        +
                        gain
                        )
                    /
                    static_cast<double>(
                        streakRsiPeriod
                        );


                averageLoss =
                    (
                        averageLoss *
                        static_cast<double>(
                            streakRsiPeriod - 1
                            )
                        +
                        loss
                        )
                    /
                    static_cast<double>(
                        streakRsiPeriod
                        );


                streakRsi[
                    static_cast<std::size_t>(
                        index
                        )
                ] =
                    CalculateRsiValue(
                        averageGain,
                        averageLoss
                    );
            }
    }


    for (
        int index = 1;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const double previousPrice =
            prices[index - 1];


        const double currentPrice =
            prices[index];


        if (
            previousPrice <= 0.0 ||
            currentPrice <= 0.0
            )
        {
            continue;
        }


        returns[
            static_cast<std::size_t>(
                index
                )
        ] =
            100.0 *
                (
                    currentPrice /
                    previousPrice
                    -
                    1.0
                    );
    }


    PercentRank(
        returns.data(),
        size,
        rankPeriod,
        percentRank.data()
    );


    for (
        int index = 0;
        index < size;
        index++
        )
    {
        const std::size_t position =
            static_cast<std::size_t>(
                index
                );


        if (
            !std::isfinite(priceRsi[position]) ||
            !std::isfinite(streakRsi[position]) ||
            !std::isfinite(percentRank[position])
            )
        {
            continue;
        }


        output[index] =
            (
                priceRsi[position]
                +
                streakRsi[position]
                +
                percentRank[position]
                )
            /
            3.0;
    }
}

============================================================
FILE: QuantEngine\QuantEngine.h
============================================================
#pragma once

#ifdef QUANTENGINE_EXPORTS
#define QUANTENGINE_API __declspec(dllexport)
#else
#define QUANTENGINE_API __declspec(dllimport)
#endif


extern "C"
{
    // ==========================================
    // BASIC MATHEMATICS
    // ==========================================

    QUANTENGINE_API double Add(
        double a,
        double b
    );


    // ==========================================
    // MEAN ESTIMATORS
    // ==========================================

    // Arithmetic mean:
    //
    // x_bar = (1 / n) * sum(x_i)

    QUANTENGINE_API double Mean(
        const double* values,
        int size
    );


    // Geometric mean:
    //
    // G = (product(x_i))^(1 / n)
    //
    // All values must be strictly positive.

    QUANTENGINE_API double GeometricMean(
        const double* values,
        int size
    );


    // Harmonic mean:
    //
    // H = n / sum(1 / x_i)
    //
    // All values must be strictly positive.

    QUANTENGINE_API double HarmonicMean(
        const double* values,
        int size
    );


    // Weighted mean:
    //
    // x_w = sum(w_i * x_i) / sum(w_i)
    //
    // Weights must be non-negative.
    // At least one weight must be positive.

    QUANTENGINE_API double WeightedMean(
        const double* values,
        const double* weights,
        int size
    );


    // ==========================================
    // DISPERSION
    // ==========================================

    // Sample variance:
    //
    // s^2 =
    // sum((x_i - x_bar)^2) / (n - 1)

    QUANTENGINE_API double Variance(
        const double* values,
        int size
    );


    // Sample standard deviation:
    //
    // s = sqrt(s^2)

    QUANTENGINE_API double StandardDeviation(
        const double* values,
        int size
    );


    // ==========================================
    // RETURNS
    // ==========================================

    // Log return:
    //
    // r_t = log(P_t / P_(t-1))

    QUANTENGINE_API double LogReturn(
        double previousPrice,
        double currentPrice
    );


    // ==========================================
    // RSI
    // ==========================================

    // Wilder RSI:
    //
    // Uses Wilder recursive smoothing.
    //
    // output must contain space for size values.

    QUANTENGINE_API void WilderRSI(
        const double* prices,
        int size,
        int period,
        double* output
    );


    // Cutler RSI:
    //
    // Uses simple moving averages of gains
    // and losses.

    QUANTENGINE_API void CutlerRSI(
        const double* prices,
        int size,
        int period,
        double* output
    );


    // Exponential RSI:
    //
    // Uses alpha = 2 / (period + 1).

    QUANTENGINE_API void ExponentialRSI(
        const double* prices,
        int size,
        int period,
        double* output
    );


    // ==========================================
    // STOCHASTIC RSI
    // ==========================================

    // Stochastic RSI:
    //
    // 100 * (RSI - rolling minimum RSI)
    //     / (rolling maximum RSI
    //        - rolling minimum RSI)

    QUANTENGINE_API void StochasticRSI(
        const double* prices,
        int size,
        int rsiPeriod,
        int stochasticPeriod,
        double* output
    );


    // ==========================================
    // PRICE STREAK
    // ==========================================

    // Consecutive positive closes:
    //
    // 1, 2, 3, ...
    //
    // Consecutive negative closes:
    //
    // -1, -2, -3, ...

    QUANTENGINE_API void PriceStreak(
        const double* prices,
        int size,
        double* output
    );


    // ==========================================
    // PERCENT RANK
    // ==========================================

    QUANTENGINE_API void PercentRank(
        const double* values,
        int size,
        int period,
        double* output
    );


    // ==========================================
    // CONNORS RSI
    // ==========================================

    // Connors RSI combines:
    //
    // 1. Price RSI
    // 2. Price-streak RSI
    // 3. Percent rank of one-period returns

    QUANTENGINE_API void ConnorsRSI(
        const double* prices,
        int size,
        int priceRsiPeriod,
        int streakRsiPeriod,
        int rankPeriod,
        double* output
    );
}
